## Archival scene 1

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u001 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*A coach arrives and stops. Baladevar, the queen and the others get down and go inside. Muthan, who drove the coach, also gets down. He takes the horse. A picture falls from it. Sukhadev notices.*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u002 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Hey—Mutha! What picture is this?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u003 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Nothing...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u004 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Hey!... Just show me; let me see...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u005 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He shows it.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u006 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Oh!... Mutha! I remember seeing a picture like this somewhere two or three weeks ago!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u007 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What you are about to see... this picture is my dream of an ideal... the dawn-star of joy... The picture you saw a week or two ago was another copy of this picture—an unfinished drawing...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u008 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Oh-ho! Was that yours too?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u009 | source:PDF 5 / printed 3 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Yes. The earlier one slipped from my hand and was stolen....! I drew another one. That... this...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u010 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
All right; all right, Mutha. If there were a woman in this world like the one in this picture, I would be the one to marry her.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u011 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Hm; why talk about something that does not exist.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u012 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
True enough—what people talk about in this world is what does not exist... All right, aren't you coming to the monastery?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u013 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Village meeting.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u014 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan's friends arrive.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u015 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**நண்:**  
What, Muthanna, all well?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u016 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What, is this some land of freedom for us to live in comfort? The man of comfort is right inside this monastery—Maykainatha Swami.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u017 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
May Ammaiyappan's grace reach the entire universe... O servants of the servants! O devotees filled with love!....... If you have questions of doubt, ask and gain clarity........

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u018 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Swami, what does `Ammaiyappan` mean.........

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u019 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Goodness, don't you understand even this, Prince? This palaiyakkarar Baladevar here is your...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u020 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Father!......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u021 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Right, and she.......?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u022 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Mother!.......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u023 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Amma...... Appa...... Ammaiyappa.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u024 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Oh! Then is it my mother and father whose hides you are wearing!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u025 | source:PDF 6 / printed 4 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Listen: just as you have an Ammaiyappan, the people of the world too have an Ammaiyappan.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u026 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Where are they, I wonder?—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u027 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
They cannot be seen by the eye!—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u028 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
When we, their children, can be seen by the eye, shouldn't the parents be visible too?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u029 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Wrong!... Those divine parents cannot be seen—they are formless—possessed of the power to create and destroy all the cosmic spheres... bearers of a thousand names... praised in many an invocatory verse... this world and the next, pleasure and pain... pillar and speck... there... here... shining everywhere... What, Sukhadev... has your confusion ended?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u030 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
It is only now beginning.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u031 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Oh, Ammaiyappa—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u032 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பலதேவர்:**  
Sukhadev, don't ask mischievous questions like this; trying to trip up holy men in this way is a great sin......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u033 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
No, Father, I only asked because I did not know. I take it back.......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s001-u034 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s001-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Good.

---

## Archival scene 2

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u001 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Maykainatha Swami......

Maykainatha Swami......

Maykainatha Swami......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u002 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Looks as though Maykainatha Swami has had quite a haul.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u003 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
They are all offerings sent by devotees......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u004 | source:PDF 7 / printed 5 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Offerings!... Yes—there is a name like that too for wages received without working...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u005 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Well—well—well! Where did you learn such a great truth? Did one of those Eighteen Siddhars you read say it?... Did Kudambai Siddhar say it?

Mutha, don't start your super-intellectual sermon......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u006 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Why do you get angry when I say something about the swami?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u007 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Hey, Mutha! This is the post office. People come here and go from here... Your babbling like this is dangerous for me... I knew something like this would happen; that is why I hesitated so much to rent you that room in my house....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u008 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
All right—all right—I've shut my mouth...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u009 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Not enough!... Tuck your tail in too...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u010 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
What is this—the Ramayana? Ravana's court?......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u011 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
All right, why are you standing here? Aren't you going to the palace to groom the horse?......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u012 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Why—are you afraid that if I stay here I'll keep talking about something or other?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u013 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
No. Shouldn't you work properly at the place that pays you wages? That's why I said it.... You're a good boy—go ruin yourself however you like. My duty matters to me. Only don't say anything about the swami. He isn't a choultry loafer like you; he is a saint renowned throughout the world.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u014 | source:PDF 8 / printed 6 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
A saint renowned throughout the world! It seems even such a great saint has not gained the power to win freedom for this Pazhuthar, which remains enslaved...?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u015 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Why should he care about the freedom of Pazhuthar?... Is he a man of narrow vision like you? His broad aim is for people to gain deliverance from this sinful world and go to the other world... 'Freedom for Pazhuthar'—some freedom!......

Let the younger queen Poongavanam marry the ruler of Vengai today; tomorrow Pazhuthar will have its freedom.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u016 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Is an unwilling marriage the price to be paid for freedom?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s002-u017 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s002-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
All right—all right—I can't talk to you! Ah, great freedom fighter!... See to your work... I'm coming.

---

## Archival scene 3

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u001 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Poongavanam is gathering fruit for Muthan.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u002 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Poongavanam—Poongavanam.......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u003 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-noncolon-delimiter -->
**பூங்;** *(source semicolon delimiter)*  
What is it, brother... what is the news?.......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u004 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
News! After being born with a brother like me, what other news could I have? Look—the king of Vengai has sent a palm-leaf message......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u005 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
As always, it is about my marriage, isn't it?......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u006 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Marriage? He has forgotten that now... The message that has come now... is for fifty thousand gold coins... an urgent order. The tribute must be paid, Poongavanam... the tribute must be paid...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u007 | source:PDF 9 / printed 7 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
This is nothing unusual, is it, brother!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u008 | source:PDF 10 / printed 8 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Usual? Poongavanam, think.... If you had agreed to marry that ruler of Vengaipuram, how many lakhs of gold would have remained with us over these fifteen years? I have poured it out and wept, into the seat of that tyrant's rule......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u009 | source:PDF 10 / printed 8 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
For that... can I change my opinion?......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u010 | source:PDF 10 / printed 8 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Opinion—what a grand opinion!.... For years the king of Vengai city tried to marry you and in the end took a vow of celibacy.... And you hate him......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u011 | source:PDF 10 / printed 8 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Brother... this is neither the time nor the age for me to talk of marriage... Please let me live in peace... I don't want that wretched marriage talk! I absolutely do not want it.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s003-u012 | source:PDF 10 / printed 8 | record:ammaiyappan-s003-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
By crying and crying like this, you've ruined Pazhuthar itself... What use is there in talking to you......

---

## Archival scene 4

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u001 | source:PDF 10 / printed 8 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Maykainatha Swami is seated on a gold-polished dais. Nearby are Baladevar, his wife and Sukhadev; the guard Tirisangu; the others stand.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u002 | source:PDF 10→11 / printed 8→9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கைநாதர்:**  
Didn't I tell you about great saints yesterday? Great saints do not appear often. But when such saints do appear, there is nothing they cannot do... They will dry up the sea; they will float in kasanthi... They will speak with God... They will make sugarcane taste bitter; they will make even a crow sing a Gandharva melody... Such are those endowed with divine grace. Warriors and men of wisdom have fame only on earth... But great saints have fame even in heaven. That is why I say: do not touch sword or spear; worship the fragrance of Vaikuntha. Do not bear a shield in your hand. Bear the sacred ash of the Lord of Kailasa... Who are you? Who am I? Mother, father, elder brother, younger brother—all are vain; in the end, a handful of earth.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u003 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Everyone receives sacred ash and leaves.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u004 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Boopathi enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u005 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Come, Boopathi... come. It has been many years since I saw you! Your beard has gone completely white.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u006 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Yes!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u007 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
A tender leaf becomes a dry leaf, after all. What is surprising about a beard turning white?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u008 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
You said one thing—and said it well. True; as clear as a gooseberry in the palm...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u009 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Well, Boopathiyar, how were your travels abroad? I imagine your medical practice has improved many times over since before—your travels were useful for that, weren't they?—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u010 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Certainly they were. Twenty years of ceaseless wandering did not go to waste—the truth that what cannot be cured by magic and mantra can be cured by medicine......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u011 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Ammaiyappa—there is nothing God cannot do.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u012 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u013 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Swami, namaste.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u014 | source:PDF 11 / printed 9 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
What is it, Vedalam?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u015 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Many offerings have come for Swamigal. I brought them...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u016 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Offerings... Long live the devotees! How much, Vedalam?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u017 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Countless gold coins.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u018 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்கை:**  
Delightful! There is the offering box.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u019 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam leaves.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u020 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Does the Swami never touch gold?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u021 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சாமி:**  
Poison—poison!!......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u022 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
And isn't this tree growing in that very poison!... Swami! I have returned from abroad; you must give me a gift—will you?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u023 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சாமி:**  
A gift? What, Boopathi?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u024 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
You must show that treasure to my eyes.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u025 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சாமி:**  
Treasure?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u026 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Not gold or diamonds, Swami... I'm asking for the secret buried within you...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u027 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சாமி:**  
Secret!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u028 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Yes. Won't you tell me at least now?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u029 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சாமி:**  
Don't take my life; you were away for twenty years—why have you come here now, man...?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s004-u030 | source:PDF 12 / printed 10 | record:ammaiyappan-s004-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
I'll tell you why I came. You too have been living for twenty years hiding that secret! Ha—ha—ha—Swami, even if a horse hides its feed in a sack while it eats, does that mean we won't know it is kol, the horse gram? Ha—ha—ha!

---

## Archival scene 5

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u001 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*A horse is eating horse gram. A boy slowly approaches, glancing around furtively... Then he puts his hand into the feed sack, takes some horse gram and eats it himself... Tirisangu comes there... A blow lands on the boy's head...*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u002 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-noncolon-delimiter -->
**திரு;** *(source semicolon delimiter)*  
You thieving donkey—who the hell are you! What are you staring at—spit it out... mm... spit!... Will you come here again, eh...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u003 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan comes and catches hold.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u004 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Why... are you hitting him?......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u005 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரு:**  
So you've come to plead for him, have you... Mutha—what a fine way you look after the horses! Every little thieving brat is coming right inside the palace to steal!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u006 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
If this hungry boy, unable to bear his hunger, ate a little horse gram—is that theft? In this world, all sorts of people steal all sorts of things...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u007 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரு:**  
Right, then. Looks as though you'll divide up the Palayakkarar's property and hand it to the hungry yourself.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u008 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Even if I don't, that time is bound to come. Here, little brother—you run along......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u009 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He takes some horse gram and gives it to him.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u010 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரு:**  
Kaveri is such a good woman—and a boy like this had to be born from her belly!......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u011 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He leaves. A warrior enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u012 | source:PDF 13 / printed 11 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீர:**  
Mutha!... Kumar Raja is coming—to learn swordsmanship.......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u013 | source:PDF 14 / printed 12 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Who's that! Kumar Raja? All right!......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u014 | source:PDF 14 / printed 12 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீர:**  
Yes! Prince Sukhadev......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s005-u015 | source:PDF 14 / printed 12 | record:ammaiyappan-s005-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Prince, Kumar Raja!... As if he's someone different—he just keeps flinging out new titles...... Hey, at least look at the merit before showering him with titles; what now... is he going to learn swordplay and win freedom for Pazhuthar?...... What a mighty hero of heroes... All right... all right... go... I'm coming.

---

## Archival scene 6

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u001 | source:PDF 14 / printed 12 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
I am a hero among heroes—let enemies come from any side; I will face them and crush them... Let armies come by the akroni count! I will smash them like pots. Let them lay siege to forts... let them enter the audience hall itself... I will resist... drive them off... strike... pound the enemies of the world to dust... reduce all the chariots, elephants, cavalry and infantry to powder. Hmm... yes... which hand holds the shield, and which hand holds the sword... Hey, just tell me that much, will you?

(Muthan enters.

Ah!... He'll know. Mutha!... Within two days I must become a great swordsman.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u002 | source:PDF 14 / printed 12 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Oh... we can make you into a swordsman.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u003 | source:PDF 14 / printed 12 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
What! A swordsman?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u004 | source:PDF 14 / printed 12 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah, it slipped out wrong. Never mind... You've heard of the sword-hero Hanuman, haven't you?......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u005 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Yes... yes, the one who went to Sita in Ashoka Grove...

All right... all right... come...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u006 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They begin swordplay; Muthan starts whirling his sword a little faster......]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u007 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[சுக — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Hmm.... Will this work with him?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u008 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They fight again.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u009 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-r002 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[சுக — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Hey... wait... wait! It'll strike somewhere it shouldn't and somebody will get killed... Give me that...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u010 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He himself goes and brings a long sword and a dagger, looking at the warrior standing there.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u011 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Hey... you go.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u012 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[To Muthan.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u013 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-r003 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[சுக — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Here... hold this... Now let's see who wins...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u014 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Wait. This is no way to conduct a fight......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u015 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
I don't need any rules.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u016 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He swings the sword at Muthan. It strikes him. Seeing this, Poongavanam runs over......]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u017 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Sukhadev!...... What is this reckless game?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u018 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Swordplay. Why are you interfering in this, aunt? Mutha! Put medicine on the wound; I'm going.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u019 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev leaves. At once Poongavanam tears a strip of cloth and binds the wound....]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u020 | source:PDF 15 / printed 13 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**(திகிலுடன்) முத்:**  
Young Queen...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u021 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
It's all right, Mutha. I'll bandage it myself.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u022 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tears well in her eyes....]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u023 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Yuva Rani, you seem somehow different...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s006-u024 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s006-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
It's nothing, Mutha... Here, eat this. I'm going—someone may come...

[She gives him the fruit she had kept hidden and leaves. Muthan stands bewildered with the fruit.]

---

## Archival scene 7

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u001 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev admires his beauty in the mirror. Tirisangu comes there with a letter.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u002 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
This mirror isn't enough to behold my beauty, haa-m—Tirisangu! A palm-leaf letter for me?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u003 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
No, thambi. It's for me.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u004 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
From whom?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u005 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
He's an idle fellow. Once day breaks, his one job is to take my life.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u006 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Take your life? Who is that Yama, man?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u007 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Ask me, thambi. I have a good-for-nothing nephew. Says he wants a woman.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u008 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Why? Are all those being born nowadays male?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u009 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
No, thambi, no. He wants a woman for marriage.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u010 | source:PDF 16 / printed 14 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
For marriage, of course you need a woman. What else do you need? That's the raw material of a wedding, isn't it?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u011 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Thambi! The mango fruit itself is calling, ‘Bats, come!’

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u012 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Yes! Yes! A ripe mango and a ruined hall don't need to call, ‘Bat, come.’ All right, likewise......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u013 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
My daughter has come of age. Fellows are shooting their bows; that's why I've written a palm-leaf letter to this useless fellow. My daughter's beauty, her character—can this fellow claim a right over her merely because of the aththan kinship, thambi?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u014 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Oh-ho! Is your daughter that beautiful—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u015 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Haven't you ever seen her?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u016 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
No. What's your daughter's name?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u017 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Muthayi—Muthayi means Muthayi.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u018 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Hmm—even a crow thinks its chick is a golden chick.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u019 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
No, thambi. This is a koel's chick that a crow has brooded.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u020 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Tirisangu! Come, let's go to your house.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u021 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Don't joke—the prince coming to a palace guard's house... what a joke, what a joke.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u022 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Don't you know I have none of that high-and-low with me? You're a servant only to my younger sister. To me, you're an elderly friend. Right?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u023 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
I am fortunate.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u024 | source:PDF 17 / printed 15 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Come, let's go.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u025 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record: | occurrence:ammaiyappan-song-001 | origin: -->
*Song / performance reference*  
> [As the Muthan–Muthayi love-song performance is nearing its end, Sumathi arrives carrying two water pots, one on either side at her waist. She sets the pots at a distance, looks at Muthan and Muthayi, covers both her eyes with her hands, and comes toward them.]

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u026 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Muthayi! Shall we go?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u027 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
What, Sumathi! Why have you closed your eyes?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u028 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Opening her eyes.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u029 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
For a reason. It's getting late. Come, let's go.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u030 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Shall I go and come back?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u031 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
You won't get just that permission over there.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u032 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Well, your friend seems to be quite a clever-handed woman.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u033 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Not just a ‘handy one’—a foot one, a neck one, a head one too.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u034 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Enough! Enough! Don't keep spitting ‘kaari, kaari’ in my face.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u035 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record:ammaiyappan-s007-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
All right! I'm coming. Hey, Sumathi! Turn that way for a moment. I'll be right there.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s007-u036 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[As Sumathi stands turned away, a sound—‘ich’—echoes on Sumathi's face.]*

---

## Archival scene 8

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u001 | source:PDF 18 / printed 16 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi comes carrying a water pot.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u002 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Muthayi!... Muthayi!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u003 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What is it, appa?......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u004 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Where did you go, girl? How many places am I supposed to search for you... See who's come... Thambi, come in. Shoo... shoo... who called you in...?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u005 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[A goat comes in; Sukhadev follows it.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u006 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[திரி — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Come in, thambi!... Don't be shy, girl... there's no one else... This is Prince Sukhadev.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u007 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Greetings!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u008 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Long life!... Long life!... Ah!... The way those hands fold and unfold looks like a lotus flower. Tirisangu, everything you said is true... Oh, poor thing! Her cheeks have reddened with shyness, like ripe chillies—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u009 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi goes inside.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u010 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Should you tease her like this the moment you see her, thambi?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u011 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Eh... what did I say?... Did I say something without realizing it?... Hmm... Tirisangu, I want to ask you one thing... you won't refuse, will you...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u012 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
You are Palayakkarar Baladevar's son... the prince... I am merely your servant. You have the right to take without even asking....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u013 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Aha-ha... tch... tch... my eyes are spinning... my ears refuse to hear... my tongue says it won't move... ayyayyo! My heart isn't beating at all......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u014 | source:PDF 19 / printed 17 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Thambi!... Thambi!... What is this... Shall I go bring the physician....?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u015 | source:PDF 19→20 / printed 17→18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
No, no. This is an illness with no medicine... ‘when eye looks with eye...’... that is, I have fallen like a fly into the toddy-pot called love!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u016 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Thambi!... Go today and come tomorrow; the matter will succeed......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u017 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
‘Go today, come tomorrow’... Ah! I feel I've heard those words somewhere... Hmm... yes, in the Kamba Ramayanam... Rama says exactly that to Ravana... Ayyo! Ravana came back the next day and did not get victory; didn't he get death instead......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u018 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
But the Rama who came the next day won, didn't he, thambi...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u019 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Oh!... Then this Rama will definitely come tomorrow...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u020 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Do come for certain...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u021 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Would I stay away?—Tirisangu's house is a lotus pond; the bee that flies there is this Sukhadev... I'm going now, Tirisangu...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u022 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
All right, thambi!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u023 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He leaves. Tirisangu, angrily:]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u024 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Muthayi!... Muthayi!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u025 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What, appa! Has that man gone?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u026 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Man?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u027 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
No... no... forgive me; I misspoke...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u028 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Forgive you?... The prince himself came to the house... Is this how you insult him?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u029 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
The prince... hmm... a bad young man...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u030 | source:PDF 20 / printed 18 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Who is bad? Is he lacking in beauty?... Lacking in status?... Foolish girl—you cannot grasp his intelligence...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u031 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Perhaps this noble man's intelligence is smaller than the point of a needle—too small to grasp! Isn't that so, appa?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u032 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Enough, stop. If he wished, even Urvashi and Rambha would come running...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u033 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
What's surprising about that, appa? Those who came running even to the sages of the royal court—would they say no if palace men called them? They'll come, appa, they'll come—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u034 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Shut your mouth, you chatterbox! I begged and coaxed him and brought him home; you sent him away with soot smeared on his face.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u035 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
No—he left too soon! Appa! Appa! If you smeared soot on his face he'd look even more handsome, like the dark-cloud-hued one.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u036 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திருசங்கு:**  
Careful—if that reaches his ears, he'll pluck out your eyes.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u037 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Is he Kannappa Nayanar?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u038 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s008-d033 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திருசங்கு:**  
Tch! An answer for every word.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s008-u039 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi laughs and runs inside.]*

---

## Archival scene 9

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s009-u001 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s009-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பலதேவர்:**  
When there are thousands of soldiers, and when a brave commander like you, Velazhagar, is here, whom is the king of Vengai city afraid of that he is increasing the army still further?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s009-u002 | source:PDF 21 / printed 19 | record:ammaiyappan-s009-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
The army is being enlarged not out of fear... but for protection, Baladevar. Siruthaiyuran is waiting for when he can pounce on Vengai city, for whatever pretext will give him a battlefield. That is why our king has ordered us to set about enlarging the army and build a barrier.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s009-u003 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s009-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பலதேவர்:**  
I am waiting to do whatever I can.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s009-u004 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s009-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
You will. But your townspeople may dislike joining the forces on the Vengai side. After all, is he not the king who has made them slaves?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s009-u005 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s009-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பலதேவர்:**  
That's nothing. I, the first slave, prostrate full-length before him. With all my heart I pray to God that he may live. As you expect, many will join the army. There is no doubt.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s009-u006 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s009-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
All right! I must go to your place.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s009-u007 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s009-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பலதேவர்:**  
The Thendral Palace on South Raja Street has been arranged for you.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s009-u008 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s009-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Excellent.

---

## Archival scene 10

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u001 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Thambi! Thambi! Ever since you came to my house... goodness! From that moment Muthayi has talked of nothing but you.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u002 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
What—what—wait, wait—is that so? Here, Tirisangu! Here—here! There are a hundred gold coins in this... keep it... hmm... what does she say—what does she say—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u003 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திருசங்கு:**  
Muthayi says, thambi, that there is bravery in your walk—a majesty in your gaze—sweetness in your speech—and she said there's something in your hand.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u004 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
A finger—a finger!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u005 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
No, thambi...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u006 | source:PDF 22 / printed 20 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
There's a ring on the finger...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u007 | source:PDF 23 / printed 21 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Not that, thambi—oh, go on—what do I know about all this wretched love-description business?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u008 | source:PDF 23 / printed 21 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Then Muthayi is in love with me. Ah, Tirisangu! If there were a father like you, and that father had a daughter like Muthayi, the world would very soon become a grove of love without opposition. Muthayi! You and I... Ayyo, I don't even know how to describe it, Tirisangu! Is there a Kamba Ramayanam in our library?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u009 | source:PDF 23 / printed 21 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
What, thambi! What connection is there between love and the Kamba Ramayanam?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s010-u010 | source:PDF 23 / printed 21 | record:ammaiyappan-s010-d010 | occurrence:ammaiyappan-song-002 | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Ayyayyo! Three-quarters of the Ramayanam is just that. Ah! Do you know how the emperor of poets, Kambar, described love?... Listen to a song, Tirisangu...

‘After “the cloud-dark one who came with the great sage”... by the fact that he was lotus-eyed... yes... her doubt fell away: “Could it be he?”... vāmamēkalaiyi vaḷarntatu...’

Hmm, gone—I forgot beyond that... Come, come, let's look it up.

---

## Archival scene 11

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u001 | source:PDF 23 / printed 21 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
You are gold that cannot be found even by searching... Father is trying to separate me from you.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u002 | source:PDF 23 / printed 21 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Your father is right to think that way, isn't he? Where is Prince Sukhadev—and where am I? I am a man who rubs down horses in the palace... motherless... not knowing who my father is... without kin or relatives... a man alone.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u003 | source:PDF 23 / printed 21 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Man alone... The stars may gather in crowds. The moon stands alone. But isn't it the moon that the water-lily waits for?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u004 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
My lily—your father dreams of becoming kin to the Palayakkarar. Is he going to give you to this paari? A dream... a dream...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u005 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
I will make even the dream come true. My dear... leave your worries... look at me; won't you look? Isn't my face medicine that can cure your worry?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u006 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan looks at her. Both smile.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u007 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Medicine indeed... my Manikkavalli! Medicine that cures worry... this guileless silver moon. How many cruel nights, drunk on the taste of sorrow, have slipped away without a word, Sundari... simply because I thought of your radiant face... I would pine, thinking, 'Am I not poor?' Then my eyes, still unable to know the limits of your beauty, would ask: 'Are you poor—you who possess a jewel no pauper or mansion-dweller could ever obtain?' My joy! You are medicine that cures my pain... a feast without equal....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u008 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They embrace.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u009 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Shh... don't go flying.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u010 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Would a skylark not fly at the touch of spring?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u011 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
One wing is not enough to fly.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u012 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
That is why I am inviting you too.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u013 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Yes! Where shall we fly?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u014 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
To the edge of the blue sky, between the beautifully arrayed clouds—above the waterfall—around the lovely grove—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u015 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She laughs.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u016 | source:PDF 24 / printed 22 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[முத்தன் — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Why are you laughing, Muthayi?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u017 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
I have heard it said... that poet... lover... madman... all three are one kind. A poet looks at the sky and the lotus and picks a quarrel: 'Hey, stars! At whom are you winking?' A lover looks at the white moon, laments, 'Where is my beloved?' and asks the moon to carry a message. People joke that both of them are mad... Looking at you, it really does seem true...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u018 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She touches him.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u019 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Hush... don't touch me... I'm the madman, after all.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u020 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Even if you are mad, aren't you my Muthan? Piththa! Pirai-kudi! Perumane!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u021 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She touches him.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u022 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Shh... amma. Wait, Muthayi.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u023 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Why! If I touch you, will it turn into sacred ash?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u024 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She touches him.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u025 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Shh... amma. Wait, Muthayi.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u026 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
That's it!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u027 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev and Tirisangu.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u028 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Ah! Right... right... maal nannoram... at the edge of the grove...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u029 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Prince! Please don't misunderstand. This fellow has started forcing himself on my girl. Hey, brainless wretch! What are you doing?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u030 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Father!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u031 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Don't be afraid, girl—he won't do anything to me. He's just a lazy fellow who frightens women.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u032 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
No, father.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u033 | source:PDF 25 / printed 23 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tirukkittu.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u034 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
I understand everything myself... Why do you come to places like this alone?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u035 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Listen to what I have to say.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u036 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
You needn't say anything; go home, girl... Don't stand in front of this sinner... it's wrong... go, girl—are you going or not? Hm... go...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u037 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi leaves, distressed.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u038 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-r002 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[திரிசங்கு — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
God! You have saved my daughter's honour... a crore salutations to you... Prince! Did you see this fellow's outrage?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u039 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is struck on the back.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u040 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Hey, Mutha! Is your back muttering? You half-starved mendicant brat! You whining donkey.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u041 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Had enough, agathi?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u042 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Don't talk... you cowardly dog! You have no way even to get half a belly of gruel, and yet you go about loving—love indeed.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u043 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Rich man's son! You who do not know the difference between the heart and the stomach! I'll teach you the grammar of love... listen... Love is not saying, 'I will bathe in rosewater, swim in fruit juice, and go to and fro from a bedchamber surrounded by maidens'... Love is a moon... a breeze... It comes not only to the mansion but to the mud hut too... Love is not money; it does not stop at your marble hall...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u044 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
That's right, fellow; you cannot hide your villainy behind too much clever talk.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u045 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d033 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
How could I? For that, one would have to be born the son of a Palayakkarar...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u046 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d034 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Shut up! You father-name-unknown mistake.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u047 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record:ammaiyappan-s011-d035 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Do you know your father's name?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u048 | source:PDF 26 / printed 24 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The two of them fight.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s011-u049 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*Sukhadev's cap flies upward and falls. A blow from Muthan knocks Sukhadev down. He gets up, bravely takes the walking stick Tirisangu is holding, advances as though to strike Muthan, retrieves his cap caught on a tree, warns Muthan, 'Hey, Mutha—careful; be careful!', and falls into the water.*

---

## Archival scene 12

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u001 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
Come... come... you're Vedalam, aren't you?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u002 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Yes.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u003 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
Why have you got a name like Vedalam? Did your father and mother read the story of Vikramaditya?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u004 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
They did; they did—why?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u005 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
There is a vedalam in the story of Vikramaditya—you must have been born while they were reading it, so they named you Vedalam. Good thing you weren't born while they were reading the Kishkindha Kandam. Come! Come!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u006 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan enters; Vedalam and the warrior bow.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u007 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
My lord! This is Vedalam, whom you sent for.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u008 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Oh! Vedalam! Come. I have heard a great deal about you. Baladevar too said you were very capable. You know why I have come—to recruit men for the army. For that, you too...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u009 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Me? Ayyo! I don't even have the strength to lift a sword.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u010 | source:PDF 27 / printed 25 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
You needn't join the army, man... Just catch and bring us some men. There must be plenty of people without work.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u011 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
But people will be afraid to join the army.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u012 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Even a goat is afraid to come to the sacrificial altar. That is why they sprinkle turmeric-water, scatter flowers, hold food out in front and tempt it; one has to make an effort.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u013 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Exactly right; I'll begin the work as you order. One more request.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u014 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
What?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u015 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
There is a great holy man in this town. Everyone regards his words as divine speech. If we ask him to campaign for men to join the army, our work will become easy.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u016 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Do we have to give him anything to receive his grace?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s012-u017 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s012-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Anything? He will never accept it. He does not even touch material wealth with his hand. There is a donation box at the monastery. It has to be put in there.

---

## Archival scene 13

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u001 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Baladevar's wife and Poongavanam are playing sokkattan. Sukhadev comes down from upstairs. Tirisangu is occupied with some work.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u002 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Tirisangu! Success! Victory... victory upon victory!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u003 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
What is it, thambi?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u004 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Father has agreed. He has given the order to dismiss Muthan from his job.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u005 | source:PDF 28 / printed 26 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்காவனம்:**  
What! Dismiss Muthan? Why? What for?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u006 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Tch... what for? He grabbed and pulled the hand of our Tirisangu's daughter, Muthayi. I rebuked him. A proper slap on the cheek.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u007 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**ராணி:**  
What?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u008 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Ah! Ah! I gave it to him.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u009 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்காவனம்:**  
Are you boasting about that? Does beating a poor man give you some thrill?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u010 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He rubs his back.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u011 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Hm—you don't know what happened, aunt. If you knew, you wouldn't talk like this.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u012 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்காவனம்:**  
Sukhadev! Don't dismiss Muthan. Who does he have apart from us? Poor orphan—where will he go?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u013 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Aunt! You, who chose not to marry and remain a maiden—why don't you think of God and roll your japa beads? Why get into all this trouble?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u014 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்காவனம்:**  
Sister-in-law, did you hear the way your son speaks?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u015 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**ராணி:**  
Sukhadev! Be quiet.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u016 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s013-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
All right, all right! Come, Tirisangu.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s013-u017 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Poongavanam looks crestfallen.]*

---

## Archival scene 14

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u001 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*Muthan is checking a sword. Poongavanam comes there....*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u002 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Mutha!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u003 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Young queen! What do you want?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u004 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Nothing... about you and Sukhadev...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u005 | source:PDF 29 / printed 27 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Yes, Rani... in a fit of anger, something happened....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u006 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Hm... who is that Muthayi?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u007 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
[Startled; silent...]

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u008 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Won't you tell me?... Who is she who has stolen your heart?... Is she beautiful?... a woman of rare virtue?... a beauty suited to you?... Won't you tell me?... Mutha!... For that girl—have you and the prince fought a great war?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u009 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
My lady!....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u010 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Do you know what your quarrel has led to?... Your very job is in danger... This sinner is going to lose even the blessing of being able to look at you...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u011 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Young queen!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u012 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Don't call me that...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u013 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev's voice is heard. Poongavanam hides. Tirisangu enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u014 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
What—oppose me?... Are there human beings in all the fourteen—sixteen worlds who can oppose me? What, Tirisangu...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u015 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Uh-uh...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u016 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Why stop at eleven worlds? There are no humans in the lunar sphere... none in the solar sphere... none in the realm of the stars...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u017 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
None, thambi... none... there isn't a single person who can oppose you...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u018 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
There is one—he too is finished; ...who?... this Muthan... Hey, Mutha!... For the crime you committed there will be no case... inquiry... judgment... none of it... accept the punishment...... You have no job here anymore...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u019 | source:PDF 30 / printed 28 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Thank you...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u020 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Thank you?... I cannot accept it. Take it back... Tirisangu... not just palace work—announce it throughout the town!... No one anywhere under our authority is to give this fellow work... If they do—I'll cut off the tail... skin the hide... break the leg... tear up the palm-leaf...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u021 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Why!... Is there no more 'O' left to say?... You could say, 'I'll tear apart the garland'!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u022 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
What... what... tear apart the garland?... Am I a monkey? Hm... hey, Mutha! Another great war between you and me is about to begin... Leave the palace immediately and respectfully!... You donkey who doesn't know your father's name!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u023 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Seeing Muthan's anger, Sukhadev quickly leaves.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u024 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Come!... Tirisangu!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u025 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan stands stunned. Poongavanam comes forward.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u026 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Mutha!... Before you go, come by my mansion and then leave!... Don't forget... you must come... you absolutely must come...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u027 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She leaves. Muthan is thoroughly confused... Poongavanam's words echo again and again in his mind...]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s014-u028 | source:PDF 31 / printed 29 | record:ammaiyappan-s014-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**
