My whole life has become a confusion...

---

## Archival scene 15

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u001 | source:PDF 31→32 / printed 29→30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**குரல்:**  
What lives here! Confusion here too! Love-stricken... a cow seeking its calf... a tiger in deerskin! How splendid the thought—how beautiful the art... Are those works of thought merely consolation to her?... Poongavanam has seen those living paintings... Yet now she keeps looking at them. Her eyes repeatedly circle back toward the doorway, listening for the sound of footsteps. Her heart beats with the longing: will Muthan come?... Ah!... there he is. (Muthan has come.)

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u002 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan enters hesitantly.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u003 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Come... Mutha, come...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u004 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Why did you call me?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u005 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
I'll tell you—come!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u006 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
No, I'll stay right here...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u007 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She runs to him.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u008 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
My dear; come near me!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u009 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u010 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Mutha... Mutha...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u011 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Young queen! This does not become your station... You, who vowed to live as an unmarried maiden, refusing marriage—are you calling me, 'My dear, come'?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u012 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Mutha!... my dear!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u013 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Enough, Yuva Rani! Don't you remember?... When my mother left me alone and devoted herself to palace work—you used to comb and adorn me as a little boy, gather me into your arms and delight in me—are you, of all people, now daring to commit this wrong?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u014 | source:PDF 32 / printed 30 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
My joy!... listen to this...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u015 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I don't want to hear it... You are the ruler's sister... and I am a horseman... Amma, I have heard the story of Raja Bhartrhari... this is even stranger than that...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u016 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Mutha?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u017 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
All right, all right... If I stay here any longer, I may be swept into some dangerous corner by this flood of desire that has lain restrained and now surges like this... Hm... If you showed this uncontrollable affection to the king of Vengai Nadu—who, unable to attain you, suffers and has taken a vow of brahmacharya—perhaps this country would at least gain freedom... I am going...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u018 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Mutha... stop...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s015-u019 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s015-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
No... a storm has entered Poongavanam; I cannot stand against it, amma, I cannot. I'm leaving!

---

## Archival scene 16

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u001 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Hm... Muthan... from now on he is only a lifeless husk—; not merely in the palace—he will get no work anywhere under the Palayakkarar's authority.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u002 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Father! What if he stays in our house until he finds work?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u003 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Exactly! I tell you not to play with your food; you say you'll bring that very thing and put it around my neck as a garland. Muthayi! Listen to me. No one would dare walk through a cactus wilderness with his eyes open.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u004 | source:PDF 33 / printed 31 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
It isn't a cactus wilderness, Father—it's a grove of love.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u005 | source:PDF 33→34 / printed 31→32 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Shut your mouth—or I'll close my own ears. Love, love, before Tirittira Narayanan; please, girl, have a little mercy. Ours is a family in debt. And that is not all. I am indebted in duty to the prince too—whenever I needed it he gave me money, and I stretched out my hand and took it—even the other day he gave a little gold for the preliminary wedding expenses.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u006 | source:PDF 34 / printed 32 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Pointing to the box.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u007 | source:PDF 34 / printed 32 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Father! Must my life be ruined for the sake of wealth? Don't I have the right to choose a man suited to my life, Father?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u008 | source:PDF 34 / printed 32 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
I have chosen a man suited to your life—with mansions and lofty towers. An iron strongbox itself is coming in search of us—and you run after a beggar's bowl.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u009 | source:PDF 34 / printed 32 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Father! I was raised in freedom. Don't turn me into a gambler grasping for the Palayakkarar's property, or into collateral used to pay off your debt, Father.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s016-u010 | source:PDF 34 / printed 32 | record:ammaiyappan-s016-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Enough, Muthayi, enough—I will never consent, while I am alive, to your garlanding that annakkavadi. If you refuse me, your wedding procession can set out only after my funeral procession has gone. Yes! Remember that.

---

## Archival scene 17

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u001 | source:PDF 34 / printed 32 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan and his friends are there.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u002 | source:PDF 34 / printed 32 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Remember this—today it is me; tomorrow it may be you; this is how we can be struck in the stomach.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u003 | source:PDF 34 / printed 32 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**நண்பன்:**  
What other way is there for this?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u004 | source:PDF 34→35 / printed 32→33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
As long as we are all slaves, there will always be a new authority each day and a petty rule for each man... After making room for the honey, can we still hope for drops of honey?... The man from Vengai Nadu threatens the Pazhuthar Palayakkarar... the Palayakkarar threatens us and vents that anger... the Palayakkarar's son crushes the roses of the house and throws them away, then conducts a king's durbar... Is it wrong for me to question that?... I am a horseman! They are lofty minds atop towers! Hm... slaves a little higher up; that is all!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u005 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The friends laugh...]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u006 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[முத்தன் — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Laughter is no use, friends—we must think. We must turn our Pazhuthar, which has become a spider's web, into a splendid flower garden!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u007 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam comes in.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u008 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Change it, man; change it!... You can't even pay the rent for this palm-sized place... and you'll turn Pazhuthar into a flower garden!... turn it into a pushpa forest too—Hey!... who are all you fellows, hm?... what work have you got here?... Hm... uff... go... go...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u009 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They leave.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u010 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-r002 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[வேதாளம் — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Hey... Mutha! First give me my rent....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u011 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Rent? You're asking at a time like this?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u012 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
When else am I to ask?.... When you have money, you give it yourself; when you don't, I have to ask and get it...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u013 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I haven't found a single job...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u014 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Yes!.... You won't get one hereafter either... so...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u015 | source:PDF 35 / printed 33 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Wait a little.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u016 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Hey, Mutha! Brainless young man! Is there no way to seek wealth in this world?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u017 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
By stealing?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u018 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Tch! You're a sturdy fellow... there's good strength in your body...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u019 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I could knock Baladevar down with one blow; is that it?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u020 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Madman!... That's not what I mean—I mean join the army of the people of Vengaipuram!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u021 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What? The army?!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u022 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Yes! The Palayakkarar's authority cannot stop it... the renowned spear... Vel the warrior...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u023 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Enough... enough... Me, in the army of the overlord who has enslaved us? Tch! Better to die!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u024 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Everyone in town is a fool and you're the only wise man?... Maykkainatha Swamiyar himself is going to campaign for the war...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u025 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
The Swamiyar! Of course he will. He isn't the one who will die in the fighting... that's why!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u026 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record:ammaiyappan-s017-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Enough... don't join the army either. I don't want anything. What is it to me? Just somehow pay my rent. Don't make a good man keep asking—I'm going... to hear the sermon.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s017-u027 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He leaves.]*

---

## Archival scene 18

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u001 | source:PDF 36 / printed 34 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The townspeople are gathered. Thanapathi, Velazhagar, Doctor Boopathi, Vedalam and many others are present. Maykkai Natha Swamigal's speech is in progress.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u002 | source:PDF 37 / printed 35 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்க்கை நாதர்:**  
Friends! Until now Thanapathi Velazhagar has delivered an instructive speech about the benefits of the army. I too am going to say the same thing. Bhagavan Paranthaman himself told Parthan to take part in the Bharata war. Those who preach today that war must not be fought, that it is a path of frenzy—let them read the Bhagavad Gita. Then they will see how much that flute-player has said about the necessity of war. What do the trident in Siva's hand, the spear of Siva's son, and Vishnu's discus signify? Peace? Or battle? Therefore, friends! All of you become warriors... do not fear death. It is not only by taking up a sword that people die; many have ended their lives by slipping on a banana peel. For a warrior, there is fame not only on earth but in heaven too. Bear the garland of victory upon those bent shoulders. For that, bear swords in your hands. Strike your strong shoulders and set out saying that God is your support... Ammaiyappa! Long live the path of virtue!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u003 | source:PDF 37 / printed 35 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மக்கள்:**  
Ammaiyappa! Ammaiyappa!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u004 | source:PDF 37 / printed 35 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The crowd disperses. Thanapathi and the Swamiyar meet in a private place.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u005 | source:PDF 37 / printed 35 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
I offer you my deepest thanks.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u006 | source:PDF 37 / printed 35 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்க்கை:**  
I did my duty.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u007 | source:PDF 37 / printed 35 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
If heads of monasteries like you set out to serve the country, both worlds will be illuminated. I will meet you later. I take my leave.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u008 | source:PDF 37 / printed 35 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்க்கை:**  
Ammaiyappa! Long live the path of grace.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u009 | source:PDF 37→38 / printed 35→36 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Maykkai Nadhar comes back near his seat. Doctor Boopathi alone is still sitting there. Seeing him, Maykkai Nadhar is startled.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u010 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்க்கை நாதர்:**  
What, Boopathi! You still haven't left?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u011 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
I forgot myself, Swamiyare! I lost my very heart in your speech. Hee... hee... hee... 'Do not touch sword or spear—worship the Lord of Vaikuntha... do not bear a shield in your hand—bear the sacred ash of the Lord of Kailasa...' Where is all that now? 'Bear the garland of victory on bent shoulders... for that, bear spears in your hands...' And where is this? Everything is maya... magic illusion... isn't it, Swamiyare?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u012 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்க்கை நாதர்:**  
Boopathi! Enough, please go.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u013 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
I am praising you, Swamiyare, praising you! Ahaha! Lord! What all they use you for. Every great man who appears in the world, they say, is your incarnation. They say you incarnated as the Paramatma and told Arjuna to wage war. Then, they say, you yourself incarnated as Bhagavan Buddha and showed Ashoka the good path, saying war is evil. When they have made even you contradict yourself like this, what does this Swamiyar amount to?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u014 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்க்கை நாதர்:**  
Boopathi! If a sadhu is enraged, even the forest cannot contain it, Boopathi!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u015 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
It is not the sadhu that is trembling... it is deceit, argument, and evil that are trembling.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u016 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்க்கை நாதர்:**  
Why should there be any quarrel between us, man....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u017 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
What is there between us? Tell me that secret.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u018 | source:PDF 38 / printed 36 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்க்கை நாதர்:**  
Boopathi: I bow to you. Please go away. Just don't ask about that secret!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u019 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s018-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Swamiyare! I'm going. Swamiyare! Remember just one thing. A lie slowly, little by little, grows and blossoms... but truth suddenly explodes... Ha... ha... ha... I'm going.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s018-u020 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Boopathi leaves. The Swamiyar lets out a deep sigh and goes to sit on the seat.]*

---

## Archival scene 19

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u001 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record: | occurrence:ammaiyappan-song-003 | origin: -->
*Song / performance reference*  
> [While Muthan is singing, the post-runner carries some bags out. Tirisangu enters.]

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u002 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Where is Muthan? Mutha!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u003 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Ah!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u004 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Don't be afraid, Mutha.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u005 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Why fear! The palace must have issued some new order.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u006 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
The palace has nothing to do with my coming... Mutha, there is no one here, is there?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u007 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
No... what is it?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u008 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
I know that there is true love between you and my daughter.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u009 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
At least you know it now!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u010 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Listen, Mutha! Muthayi has said she cannot marry anyone but you. She has fallen to the bow you shot.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u011 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
I did not shoot, and she did not fall... We are both doves caught in the net of love!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u012 | source:PDF 39 / printed 37 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
That net must be cut and my daughter freed. Even after she is freed, you must not remain a vulture chasing her.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u013 | source:PDF 40 / printed 38 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
She will not be at a distance that needs chasing. Muthayi is a parrot perched on my shoulder! Are you thinking, 'What is this—this son-in-law speaks to his father-in-law without any shyness?' Forgive me.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u014 | source:PDF 40 / printed 38 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Listen, Mutha; you are the one who must save my honour. Do you know how I wish my daughter to live?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u015 | source:PDF 40 / printed 38 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Our life will go exactly as you wish!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u016 | source:PDF 40→41 / printed 38→39 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
It will not, Mutha. It will not. Do not ruin a family for nothing. You have no means to live; you cannot live. Leave me aside... Would any father, however mad, agree to give his daughter to a poor man like you? Can you run a family? You are a man too; have you lost all shame, to marry and then lie waiting upon your father-in-law's house? Muthayi is a young girl. She does not know the world...... She talks of love now. After marriage, all those love-songs will become stories of the past. Diamond earrings for the ears, a pearl necklace for the neck—that is how women's minds change. Can you buy those for my daughter? Leave running a family aside—do you even have one gold coin to fix the marriage and give the priest dakshina? Mutha! Don't think I am speaking in anger; I will tell you one more thing. You worked at the post-house. That too is gone... You groomed horses for the post-house and, as wages for that, lived in this palm-sized room. You cannot do even that work anymore; the palace order forbids it. From now on the shade of houses and the shade of cattle—those are your dwellings! Mutha! Do not grieve that I am examining your poverty; along with all this grief I will tell you one more thing, so that you may understand. Suppose you were older, used to living in step with the world, and suppose you too had a daughter. Would you give that daughter in marriage to a bridegroom whose father's name was unknown? Perhaps you might. Could I? Would my orthodox mind allow it? Think! You know only that your mother was Kaveri; whom she bore you to is......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u017 | source:PDF 41 / printed 39 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Enough! Enough, sir, enough!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u018 | source:PDF 41 / printed 39 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Covering your ears is no use. Opening your eyes wide is no use. Open the door of thought... open it just a little; that is enough. Prosperity, distinction, and a splendid mansion are calling my daughter. Will you make her forget them? Will you, Mutha? If you refuse, that is not a sign of your love for her; it is a sign of your cruel wish to ruin her. Mutha! I beg you. If need be, I will fall at your feet. Forget her... forget her.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u019 | source:PDF 41 / printed 39 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
[Crying] Ah! Ayyo! A jewel that cannot be forgotten, a treasure that cannot be lost.... am I to forget that?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u020 | source:PDF 41 / printed 39 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
If you do not forget her, Muthayi must forget her father. If she forgets him like that, the corpse of this Tirisangu, hanging orphaned in mid-air from a tree branch, will curse you... the corpse will curse you!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u021 | source:PDF 41 / printed 39 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Sir! You ask me to forget my very life. I will forget it.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u022 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Mutha!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u023 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s019-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Go on, then.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s019-u024 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tirisangu leaves, pleased. Muthan is thrown into confusion.]*

---

## Archival scene 20

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u001 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Men are standing in a line to join the army. One man signs before Thanapathi. Vedalam stands nearby, with Kurumban opposite him.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u002 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**குறும்பன்:**  
Hm... the signature is done. It means you have joined the army.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u003 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The next man comes forward to sign.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u004 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
If you withdraw after this, you must pay a fine of one hundred gold coins.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u005 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**ஆள்:**  
Why would I withdraw!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u006 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
What is your name?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u007 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**ஆள்:**  
Samasam!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u008 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Are you joining the fighting?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u009 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**ஆள்:**  
Yes.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u010 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**குறும்பன்:**  
Father's name?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u011 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**ஆள்:**  
Dina Dayalar; mother, Anbukarasi.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u012 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Sign! (Thanapathi rises; as he looks toward the others, the line of men is shown.)

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u013 | source:PDF 42 / printed 40 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Sabash! Vedalam! You have brought quite a lot of men today.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u014 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Yes! Those who make the effort do not suffer disgrace, my lord.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u015 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam points to a man and touches him.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u016 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
He is very brave.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u017 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Is that so! A proper place is waiting in the army.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u018 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Have you seen this fellow, my lord? A skull that will not crack even after a hundred blows. Plenty of head-weight.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u019 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[A man nearby.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u020 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**ஆள்:**  
Then he could even become war minister.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u021 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam strikes him on the chest.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u022 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
His firmness of heart is tremendous, my lord.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u023 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The two keep laughing. Suddenly Vedalam looks over. Muthan is standing there. Vedalam is startled.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u024 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Who—Muthan?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u025 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Yes!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u026 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
You......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u027 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
I have come to join the army.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u028 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Besh! Besh! Pride is overflowing, my lord! He is a very good man. Him too... Mutha! Come... come... sign.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u029 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam runs to the table, brings the registers that must be signed, and holds them out to Muthan.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u030 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Hm... sign here.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u031 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
What is your name?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u032 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Muthan.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u033 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Hm... father's name?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u034 | source:PDF 43 / printed 41 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan stares blankly.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u035 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Hm... that isn't necessary, my lord.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u036 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Oh! At least your mother's name...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u037 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
My mother was Kaveriyammal. She died.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u038 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Hm... sign...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u039 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He signs, mistakenly writing 'Muthayi'.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u040 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s020-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
What? Muthayi? Ha... ha... ha... write it without fear.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s020-u041 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan signs.]*

---

## Archival scene 21

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u001 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi and Sumathi enter carrying water pots.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u002 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Poor thing... after not seeing her beloved for two or three days, she must have been in terrible distress... Come, let's see whether her bangles are slipping off.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u003 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Sumathi, enough!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u004 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Why—does it hurt more the more I talk about it?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u005 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
You would know what kind of pain it is only if you experienced it yourself.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u006 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Lord! Let experiences like this never come to me.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u007 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Even the Lord has had such an experience, girl.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u008 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u009 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
There—your lover has come.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u010 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
All right, all right... Sumathi, run along.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u011 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Let it be... I'm not ashamed of anything.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u012 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
We are, girl... go away.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u013 | source:PDF 44 / printed 42 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
All right! I'm going... come back before I finish bathing.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u014 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
I'll keep bathing only until you return... Is your body made of jaggery, that it will dissolve in the water?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u015 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
My body isn't jaggery... Just wait till he comes and describes it; then you'll know whose body is jaggery.... [in a man's voice] Rock candy! Honey! Boiled milk!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u016 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Go away, chatterbox.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u017 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi raises her hand. Sumathi runs off. Muthan arrives looking dejected.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u018 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Why do you look troubled? Eat this.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u019 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
No.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u020 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Oh! You haven't come for two days—are you getting in first because you think I'll be angry?

I'm not angry at all. They say that when the ink-writing stylus in one's hand comes close to the eyebrow, it disappears from sight. In the same way, once you come close to me, all the wrongs you have done disappear from my sight.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u021 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Muthayi! You grieve like this over only two days apart—I'm afraid even to think of those dark days when we may not be able to meet at all.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u022 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
What... what are you saying?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u023 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
My grove-cuckoo! I have become a desert where you cannot sing.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u024 | source:PDF 45 / printed 43 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
If you become a desert, I will be the date palm that grows in it.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u025 | source:PDF 45→46 / printed 43→44 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Don't play, Muthayi, don't play... blinking your eyes like stars, showing pearly white teeth, showing the gait of a spotted peacock that comes sweeping in,
playing your games, playing in my lap, O `magame` dancing in my eyes! Cluster of gem-like flowers! The time has come when all that play must be buried under the earth, my dear.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u026 | source:PDF 46 / printed 44 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
What... who is talking like this... my Muthan? My Muthan?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u027 | source:PDF 46 / printed 44 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Not your Muthan... Muthan is not speaking; the warrior of Vengaipuram is speaking, my dear—the warrior of Vengaipuram is speaking.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u028 | source:PDF 46 / printed 44 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Ah! A warrior of Vengaipuram!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u029 | source:PDF 46 / printed 44 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Yes!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u030 | source:PDF 46 / printed 44 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
What do you mean, yes?... Whom did you ask before turning yourself into a warrior of Vengaipuram?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u031 | source:PDF 46 / printed 44 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Whom did I ask?... I asked poor Muthan... He said Muthayi is meant to be queen of a palace... give up thoughts of her.... The Muthan in love shed tears... then, in a `kāgaratha` voice, in a `kambiya` tone, poor Muthan gave the order: forget Muthayi... I forgot her... I forgot my beauty... I forgot the treasure that had taken residence in my thoughts... There was no other way to keep the promise I gave your father... If there had been another way, would I have joined the army of the enemy that keeps Pazhuthar enslaved?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u032 | source:PDF 46→47 / printed 44→45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
What crime did I commit that you should forget me, other than being born the daughter of a greedy father?... What is love? A mirror that reflects the form of whoever stands before it? A picture scratched with a line? I thought love was an indelible painting—an unmoving Himalaya. Was that only madness? What difference is there between my father, who has treated me as market goods, and you? Have they made me into a statue?
My story will not end with, 'The lover forgot; the beloved flew elsewhere,' my lord... You are trying to rewrite the Ambikapathi epic. It is not only the king's daughter Amaravathi who has a heart for love; the slave's daughter Muthayi has one too! I will show it to you as a spectacle—there, on that swaying tree branch... in that rushing flood... be ready to see my corpse in one of the two!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u033 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Muthayi; I have already registered my name in the army... what can I do?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u034 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
So what? I know you can withdraw by paying a fine of one hundred gold coins.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u035 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
A fine?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u036 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d033 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Why—do you say paying a fine is shameful? It is no greater shame than going as a mercenary soldier for an enemy country... This is not a fine paid out of fear of prison.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u037 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d034 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Muthayi!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u038 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d035 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Listen to me; Father has put aside one hundred gold coins for my marriage and locked them away without anyone knowing. What else are they for, if not this? I'll send them through Sumathi. Take them, pay the fine, and withdraw.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u039 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d036 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Listen to this...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u040 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d037 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
I won't listen to anything... you must withdraw... otherwise I...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u041 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d038 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Muthayi! Withdraw?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s021-u042 | source:PDF 47 / printed 45 | record:ammaiyappan-s021-d039 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Not from me... from the army. Eat this.

---

## Archival scene 22

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u001 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan and Vedalam are talking.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u002 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Your suspicion was perfectly right. After all, isn't he a commander of a country? You said that when he signed, he mistakenly wrote 'Muthayi'; that was not a mere slip. There really is something behind it.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u003 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
See? I knew it then itself. My Vedalam; there must be some connection between the two, mustn't there?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u004 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
There will be, there will be; certainly there will be!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u005 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
This woman he is connected with—is she a beauty with a slender waist?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u006 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
She is beautiful. Very beautiful.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u007 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
When I was staying in Chandanacheri, an intimate friend once introduced me to a beauty just like that.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u008 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Is that so?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u009 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
Though he was a friend, for arranging that fine opportunity we rewarded him with twenty gold coins, my lord,

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u010 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Gold alone? I made him the head of that very cheri.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u011 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Oh-ho!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u012 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
If possible, let's try to meet Muthayi once. What do you say, Vedalam?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u013 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
That is exactly what I have been thinking.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u014 | source:PDF 48 / printed 46 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
His face itself says he's thinking, my lord.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u015 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Vedalam; when it comes to understanding a hint and acting on it, you have no equal.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u016 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u017 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
What is it, man? What do you want?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u018 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
You must forgive me.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u019 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Tell me the matter.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u020 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
The circumstances are not right; I came to withdraw from the army...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u021 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Are you playing games? Who do you think I am? You cannot join the army and leave it whenever you please here! Look at the fellow—built like a wrestler... afraid like a rat, coward...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u022 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Please do not use that word. I may be poor. But I am not a coward.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u023 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Don't stretch out your tail! The sword will stretch out. Stand respectfully! Speak respectfully.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u024 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
There is no compulsion to join the army, is there?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u025 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
There is no compulsion to join, but once you have joined, leaving is not up to you.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u026 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
That is why there is a condition that one must pay a fine of one hundred gold coins.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u027 | source:PDF 49 / printed 47 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
[To Vedalam] This arrogant fellow is reminding me of the condition. [To Muthan] What are you going to do by building up that body? I asked for a sacrifice for the country.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u028 | source:PDF 49→50 / printed 47→48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
I can sacrifice myself—if we had a country of our own! If war broke out to defend our land, Muthan's name would be first in the front line. Pazhuthar is enslaved soil; you are the commander of the country that guards it, tramples it, and exacts tribute from it;
for me to serve in that army is all the same...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u029 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
What did you say, traitor to the king?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u030 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Even a betrayer of his own brethren has been given the title `Alvar`. Commander, I am no traitor! Is it treason to say that I will not serve in the ranks of those who seized our land?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u031 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Ah! He is the first man who has spoken against me. I will not let him go without finding an end for him. Poison-seeds like this, sprouting against the rule of Vengaipuram, must be plucked out at the very shoot; he says he has a country of his own, and that he will work only for that country's freedom. He wanders outside when he ought to be in a madhouse.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u032 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
So that people like you can be treated;

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u033 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Tch! Savage!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u034 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Savage! Say that word to yourself while looking in a full-length mirror!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u035 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan becomes agitated and trembles.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u036 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d033 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Please come this way for a moment.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u037 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The two move aside to speak privately.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u038 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d034 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Don't let the fellow go. If you do, he'll pay the fine and escape. Then we won't be able to get hold of Muthayi.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u039 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d035 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
What can we do?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u040 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d036 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Put the donkey under guard... but one thing... I will behave as though I am kind to this puppy. I mean, I'll act the part.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u041 | source:PDF 50 / printed 48 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The two return to Muthan, speaking as they approach.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u042 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d037 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Vedalam! This is a military matter; there is no place here for your recommendation.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u043 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d038 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Please forgive him... Muthan is poor.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u044 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d039 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Vedalam! I keep telling you again and again. You must not interfere in this.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u045 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d040 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Why shouldn't I interfere? Once he gives you the hundred gold coins he owes, what can you do to Muthan? Why, Mutha! Give the hundred gold coins.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u046 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d041 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
I'll put them into your hands.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u047 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d042 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Ah! Commander! Muthan may go now, may he not?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u048 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d043 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
No... until the fine money arrives, he must remain right here.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u049 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d044 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Sir! Honoured lord! Let him go. I myself will bring the gold tomorrow morning.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u050 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d045 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Impossible... Velazhagan gives the order. Hey, who's there! Put him under guard.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u051 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d046 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Is this what your government's camp-justice says? Very fine.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u052 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d047 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Do not let him speak. Put this rascal in the cage and lock it.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u053 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is handcuffed. Vedalam covers his face and starts to leave, looking sorrowfully at Muthan as he goes.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u054 | source:PDF 51 / printed 49 | record:ammaiyappan-s022-d048 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Mutha! Karma from a previous birth does not spare even a crowned king.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s022-u055 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam leaves. Muthan is locked in a room of Thendral Palace, with a guard posted outside.]*

---

## Archival scene 23

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u001 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi enters with a bag of gold and sees Sumathi.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u002 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record:ammaiyappan-s023-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Sumathi! Come, come! You have come at a good time!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u003 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record:ammaiyappan-s023-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
What—is it Amrita Yoga?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u004 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record:ammaiyappan-s023-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Hey, Rahu Kalam! Listen to what I'm saying!... There are one hundred gold coins in this. Somehow they must reach him...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u005 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record:ammaiyappan-s023-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
To Muthan, of course...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u006 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record:ammaiyappan-s023-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Who else—? Go quickly, Sumathi!......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u007 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record:ammaiyappan-s023-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Ah... ah!... What a hurry!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u008 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sumathi leaves.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s023-u009 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record:ammaiyappan-s023-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
[To herself] Once he gets the gold, my beloved will leave the army... once he leaves, somehow we will plead with Father and have our marriage performed. After the marriage... ah! My heart grows sweet just thinking of that feast...

---

## Archival scene 24

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u001 | source:PDF 52 / printed 50 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is confined in Thendral Palace. Vedalam enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u002 | source:PDF 52→53 / printed 50→51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Mutha! What foolishness you have done! Couldn't you have thought first before acting? Did I force you into the army? Velazhagan is a brute. You, poor fellow, are very good. By rights you should not be kept under guard. Who asked for this injustice? I ran to the Palayakkarar and told him. He paid no heed to what I said.
How can a poor man's voice be heard in a seven-storied palace?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u003 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u004 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
What, Vedalam! What are you muttering? If you wish, you may look at him and then go. You have no permission to speak with him. Muthan is now a caged bird.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u005 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Muthan used to be with me, my lord. Exalted sir! Old affection is a terribly binding thing, isn't it! Somehow forgive him......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u006 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழ:**  
Ha... ha... forgive him? Look at this government order. The king has commanded that I leave immediately for Tilankar with the soldiers enlisted so far. We must leave today.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u007 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Muthan too?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u008 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழ:**  
Yes! But if the hundred-gold fine arrives, he can be released... Hm... where would he get a hundred gold coins... a man who lies in the rubbish!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u009 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan leaves. Vedalam acts so that Muthan will trust him.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u010 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Hm. Mutha! It pains me even to look at you, man.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u011 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Sir! Will you do me a favour?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u012 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
What favour? Tell me... I'll see it done.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u013 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
A hundred gold coins will be sent to me. Take them, pay the fine, and somehow get me released.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u014 | source:PDF 53 / printed 51 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
What other work have I? The gold will certainly come, won't it?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u015 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
It will certainly come.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s024-u016 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s024-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Mutha! There is nothing to fear now. Stay without worry. Whatever happens, let the gold come. I will take care of everything.

---

## Archival scene 25

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u001 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sumathi enters with a bag of gold.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u002 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s025-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Hey, you puppy of a fellow, what are you doing outside... go... inside!... Who are you, girl? What do you want?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u003 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s025-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Oh... you!... Hm. Have you seen Muthan?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u004 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s025-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
I too have been wandering around for Muthan's sake.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u005 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s025-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சும:**  
I need to see him.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u006 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s025-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Mm-hm. You can't see him at all... they have put him in the camp prison. To get him released, one hundred gold coins are needed...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u007 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s025-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சும:**  
That is what I have brought... Here—could you please give this to him?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u008 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record:ammaiyappan-s025-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Could I give it to him?... What other work have I? Dear girl! You have done a good deed... on Muthan's behalf, my thanks to you... I'll go right now and free him...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s025-u009 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam leaves.]*

---

## Archival scene 26

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u001 | source:PDF 54 / printed 52 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan's soldiers are preparing to leave for Vengaipuram with bundles and chests. Seeing the door of his cage being opened, Muthan looks at the soldier.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u002 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
What! Am I being released?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u003 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
(Coming in laughing) Yes, release—release from here for you, you wastrel! Do you know where you must go after this release? Not home—Vengaipuram!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u004 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What! Why are you treating me so cruelly? Isn't it only just that you take your fine and release me?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u005 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Fine money—you who have no way to get even half a coin, where would you get fine money? Ha...ha...ha...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u006 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
If you keep me locked in prison like this, how can I pay your fine? Let me out... I'll bring it quickly.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u007 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Impossible—it has to come here! Even if it comes now, fine; I'll let you go.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u008 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam arrives.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u009 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
(To Vedalam) What happened? Did you get it? Has it arrived?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u010 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Ha ha—hm! Waiting and waiting, my legs are worn out. My eyes too are weary. Nothing came of it. (Turning toward Velazhagan) My lord! The fine money may arrive soon. Delay a little....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u011 | source:PDF 55 / printed 53 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
The red sun does not wait because the rooster has not crowed. I must do my duty—or the ruler will rage... hm... we must leave. Freedom fighter! You too, walk behind.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u012 | source:PDF 55→56 / printed 53→54 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I will not walk! Freedom fighters who have resolved to turn your very heads into red-lotus buds in the mire of blood will not walk like sheep behind tyrants drunk on domination! Pazhuthar is my land—a honeycomb to me; you are a forest of fire. Even if a charnel ground comes, I will say so be it; I will not follow behind you, a storm that has entered a flower garden! I am a citizen of this beautiful land—you are a lord who lives by plundering it; even a man deprived of reason would not accept your new scheme—your philosophy—that a deer should walk behind a tiger!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u013 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Enough talk... Who's there! Shackle this mightiest of heroes! Let the massed army march in front as our column moves on—and let this slave-dog be dragged behind.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u014 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u015 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record:ammaiyappan-s026-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Hm... at once...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s026-u016 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is shackled.]*

---

## Archival scene 27

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u001 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan and Vedalam are riding in a chariot when they spot Muthayi.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u002 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Kurumba!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u003 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Stop!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u004 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The chariot stops.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u005 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
My lord! There... that golden duck is the Muthayi I told you about!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u006 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Ah!... You call her a golden duck... in a poet's tongue, call her a swan... say sornam, Vedalam—I must possess her somehow... Muthayi and I must soon sing a love song together!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u007 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Don't worry... I'll be the rhythm for your song!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u008 | source:PDF 56 / printed 54 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The chariot goes on. Muthan is dragged along behind it.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u009 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi runs up and looks at Kurumban.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u010 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Sir... sir!... You're a soldier of Vengai Nadu, aren't you?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u011 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**குறு:**  
Yes... what do you want?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u012 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Have they released him?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u013 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**குறு:**  
Whom?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u014 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Muthan!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u015 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**குறு:**  
Oh!... They've released him all right!... They've released him and are bringing him there with magnificent honours!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u016 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah!... What?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u017 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi runs toward them joyfully. Muthan is lying on the ground after being beaten.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u018 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Beat me!... Beat me well!... Beat me till the spark of uprising surges and surges in my heart!... It is by beating people who committed no wrong, again and again, that the durbar halls of many self-made tyrants have been levelled to the ground, sir!... Beat me, you sinners, beat me!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u019 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi parts the crowd and sees Muthan.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u020 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Aththan!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u021 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Muthayi!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u022 | source:PDF 57 / printed 55 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Aththan... Aththan!... What have they done to you?... Even after I told you so much, you didn't listen!... Alas, Aththan... how many days had you been waiting for me to see this disfigured sight?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u023 | source:PDF 57→58 / printed 55→56 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Don't cry, Muthayi, don't cry!... This is no disfigured sight... In the killing hall of savages who live by trusting knife-points and dagger-swings, how many heroes have offered up their very lives... With swelling shoulders and the sword of freedom, never lowering the raised hand, even knowing they might perish... not a drop of fear touching their light-filled eyes, declaring, 'Either the sweet fragrance of marriage will be ours—or our corpses will stink on that field,' vowing against the enemy, showing the army the way and setting forth—how many, how many princes of freedom... sleep in the lap of Mother Freedom as children of the tomb, treasures of the grave! When I think of the scars borne by those heroic martyrs, my dear, this punishment is very little!....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u024 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan arrives.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u025 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
What's this here... who is this moonlike maiden? Who are you, girl?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u026 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Traitor... don't touch her!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u027 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Why, are you the owner of this peacock-plumed beauty?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u028 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Madman!... uncultured beast who doesn't know how to behave with women!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u029 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
What? (He slaps him)—Muthayi? Aththan!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u030 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Dishonourable man who barged into our blissful life where love and duty are woven together!... Mighty hero who takes pleasure in beating a man who lies bound!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u031 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They laugh.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u032 | source:PDF 58 / printed 56 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[முத்தன் — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Laughter!... laughter!... Laugh well!... Laugh loudly, loudly!... Velazhagan's gang that has spun a spiderweb over a painted grove... pack of wastrels... laugh well!... Ha...ha...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u033 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan falls while laughing... They drag him away.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u034 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Aththan... Aththan... Sir!... Good men, let him go! Don't take away the star of my heart!... What sin will you find in our love that even death cannot separate? Please, give my life back to me! Sir... sir... you will earn a crore blessings... let my Aththan go... Aththan!... Aththa!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u035 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tirisangu arrives.]*
