<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u036 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-r002 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[திரிசங்கு — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Shameless girl!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u037 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s027-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Aththan... Aththan!... Father!... Father!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s027-u038 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tirisangu drags Muthayi away.]*

---

## Archival scene 28

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u001 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi is in the backyard. Sumathi arrives.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u002 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Who? What brought you here?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u003 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Muthayi!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u004 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Sinner! If you had asked for it, why only a hundred—I would somehow have found even five hundred gold for you......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u005 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சும:**  
What—what are you saying?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u006 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Do I have to say it even more plainly? You act well, actress! For the sake of a miserable hundred gold, you've cut our love to pieces!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u007 | source:PDF 59 / printed 57 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சும:**  
Was I greedy for gold? Me? Muthayi! Listen to me... Just as you said, I went looking for Muthan at the post-house. He wasn't there.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u008 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
He wasn't there—you swallowed what you did get.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u009 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சும:**  
Let me speak a little too.......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u010 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Enough talking. Talking is useless. Go... go away... hm... go away, sinner... go......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u011 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சும:**  
Even if you don't understand me now, will you never understand someday? I'm leaving.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s028-u012 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s028-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Don't ever come back—traitor!

---

## Archival scene 29

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u001 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Muthayi and I were joined in a love marriage. She swore on God that she would never forget me.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u002 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Is that so!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u003 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
We ran in the pleasure garden—we bathed in the river—I said, 'Muthayi'—she called, 'My lord'—she moved her eyes... a diamond necklace at her throat—her waist swayed... I fastened a gem-studded ottiyanam around her... wearing jewels, she smiled... Singari! Oyyari! My dear! Peacock! I said, 'Come to the bedchamber'—she agreed to come... and then I woke up... it was all a dream, Tirisangu! All a dream!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u004 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
It won't remain a dream, brother—not while this Tirisangu is here!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u005 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
The gold and diamonds are ready... but my father must agree to turn them into a chain....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u006 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
That's where we have to struggle a little.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u007 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Struggle?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u008 | source:PDF 60 / printed 58 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Yes!... For Arjunan to marry Pambarani Alli... for Velan, as an old man, to marry Valli...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u009 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
I don't know what I'll have to become!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u010 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
You must become the next Palayakkarar... what doubt is there?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u011 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Hey! Vedalam! You've come at just the right time, like Vandi Rappa Nadhar... sit down there.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u012 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
No matter—it's all right—what secret consultation is going on?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u013 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
I'll put it briefly; Tirisangu's daughter Muthayi and I are in love! It has become very serious now!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u014 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
You can tell just by looking.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u015 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Father won't agree to my marrying Muthayi—do you know why?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u016 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
She's your father... that's why!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u017 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Vedalam! At this important moment, give me a first-rate idea.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u018 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Every road of this Vedalam is a shortcut. Prince! I'll arrange the marriage. If we somehow finish the wedding by trickery, won't father have to accept it afterward? Hm... yes, that's right... Prince! Take out five hundred gold... hm... quickly, Yuvraj!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u019 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Hm... here you are. (He gives it.)

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u020 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Sabash! Now it's as good as the marriage is done... Tirisangu! Come with me. I'll explain all the arrangements—what must happen and how. I'm leaving, Kumar Raj!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u021 | source:PDF 61 / printed 59 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
I'm coming, brother!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u022 | source:PDF 61→62 / printed 59→60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Tirisangu! Vedalam! Hm... in the hurry, don't forget to make the thali. Do that first, then let the other arrangements proceed; yes!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u023 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi is sitting sadly. Tirisangu arrives.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u024 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Muthayi! Muthayi! My girl, why are you sitting so sadly?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u025 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
You're asking the reason?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u026 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
I'm going through all this trouble so that you can be happy.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u027 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Father! Enough! [She cries.]

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u028 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
What has happened now, my girl? Take heart!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u029 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What is the use, father, of cutting the root of the jasmine vine and then watering its flower?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u030 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
I won't cut the root, my girl. Cheer up; there is also going to be a guru-puja at the monastery. The whole town will be in celebration. You too should be joyful...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u031 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Even beside the light, a shadow remains dark. However many joyous events there are, what medicine is there for my broken heart, father?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u032 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Don't worry! Everything will happen as you wish. Forget the wrongs I committed. Ruined by greed for wealth, I behaved like a madman. Forgive this traitor who tried to tear apart your pure love, my girl.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u033 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Father! Father!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u034 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d033 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Don't cry! I've decided to unite you and Muthan.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u035 | source:PDF 62 / printed 60 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d034 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What are you saying, father?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u036 | source:PDF 62→63 / printed 60→61 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d035 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Listen, my girl—the Palayakkarar has learned that you love Muthan. The Palayakkarar, who does not want a marriage between Sukhadevan and you, has ordered that you be married to Muthan at once.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u037 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d036 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah! Their selfish interest has wiped away my sorrow.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u038 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d037 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
The Palayakkarar has asked for Muthan to be released. They too will let him go.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u039 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d038 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Will he come?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u040 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d039 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
He won't come here... know why? Sukhadevan has decided to abduct you and marry you. To escape that, the Palayakkarar's idea is that the two of us should immediately go to a town called Anandapuram. Muthan will come there. Your marriage will be completed at once. The Palayakkarar's men are taking care of all the arrangements for your good.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u041 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d040 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Father! I can't believe it myself! I bow to you for bringing such joyful news. If I spoke rashly in any way, forgive me, father.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u042 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d041 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
All right, my girl! Let's immediately make arrangements to leave for Anandapuram...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s029-u043 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s029-d042 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What other work do I have that matters more than that, father?

---

## Archival scene 30

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u001 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Pazhuthar soldiers are cooking. Muthan arrives.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u002 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன் 1:**  
Brother Muthan!... Come, brother, come!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u003 | source:PDF 63 / printed 61 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Hm... you who should be cooking up a free kingdom in Pazhuthar... are cooking food in the enemy's land......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u004 | source:PDF 64 / printed 62 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன் 2:**  
Hm... what can we do?... It was the time our mothers gave birth to us!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u005 | source:PDF 64 / printed 62 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன் 3:**  
It's written on our heads that we must live as slaves!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u006 | source:PDF 64 / printed 62 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Don't blame the time of your birth, brother!... Our mothers never had the concern that we should all live as freedom fighters... hm... that was in the age when the mothers of Purananuru lived...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u007 | source:PDF 64 / printed 62 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன் 4:**  
Purananuru... you keep saying Purananuru—what is it, brother?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u008 | source:PDF 64→65 / printed 62→63 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
It is the heroic tale of the mothers of Tamilakam... I myself have written a poem about it in simple style... I'll tell it; listen....

Look at the hut! On one side, sharp spears and swords stand in rows. They gleam and flash like weapons forged to seize the world and end fierce enmity... Beauty in a tiger's cave is no wonder!

A dwelling of honour—a warrior's mansion! At the doorway of the house, an old woman with great grey hair opens her toothless mouth, mixes a little jaggery with rice from a bowl, and puts in a handful of food...

A warrior comes running. 'A message, grandmother,' he says... 'You are panting like a little girl who came dancing; are you a man, brother?... Let there be a space between breath and breath; then begin your speech,' says that aged Tamil woman famed for her teasing... 'This is no time for jest, grandmother; leave your usual mockery.'

'Your son fell on the battlefield,' he says... The old mother, her heart broken, straightens herself once... When grief plays its game, pieces are cut; the battlefield too is such a game... the wound—'Was it on the chest or on the back?' she asks! 'On the back,' he says... The old woman trembles; her heart bursts; she seizes the sword and speeds toward the direction where the war-drum sounded... 'Did I nurse a coward—a dullard lying face-down who bears the name warrior!... Once, to answer a spear that came flying, my deathless-eyed husband showed his chest and fell—was this one born to him?... Alas, where is honour?... He has fallen lower than the wall of this hut... On the Himalayan frontier valour laughs; here, in useless nerves, music throbs—and it too cries honour, honour! Are you from a shameless lineage that lives on maduvum and suruvum?... Hey! You have cast a stain upon the martial Tamil clan... You ate and grew fat?—where are those proud shoulders?... Did they not itch for battle—where are they?' she cries. The poor old woman nearing eighty goes to the fierce battlefront and turns over the red-Tamil bull lying shattered there... There, a flood of blood had run to guard our good Tamil land! She walks panting through heaps of corpses... Even at a wedding pavilion there was no such joy... even when her son was born, joy had a limit. He lay dead, his chest offered to the spear! 'I had been ready to cut off the breast he drank from—alas, where is the wretch who told such an unthinkable lie?... Here is the sword... where is his tongue?'

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u009 | source:PDF 65 / printed 63 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரர்கள்:**  
Tch... tch... tch...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u010 | source:PDF 65 / printed 63 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Now tell me... how precious are honour and valour... can we allow them to be trampled under the enemy's feet?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u011 | source:PDF 65 / printed 63 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரர்கள்:**  
No!... No!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u012 | source:PDF 65 / printed 63 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Then unite under my leadership to free Pazhuthar.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u013 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
Brother! This younger brother is always ready to serve under your leadership...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u014 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரர்கள்:**  
Ready.

I'm ready.

I'm ready too.

I'm ready too....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u015 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Velazhagan arrives laughing.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u016 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Everyone is ready... but I am the one who is not ready... Let you freedom fighters out, eh?... Slaves! Sitting in the mouth of a mountain python, are you blowing the magudi of freedom?... I'll throw you right into its belly... Look... can you see the prison that is going to torture you?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u017 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Prison!... You don't understand revolutionary poetry!... Know this... a flower grove where the mango-cuckoo sings... the prison that thinks to trap us...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u018 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Don't talk back... shut your mouth!......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u019 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He drags Muthan and pushes him inside.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u020 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record:ammaiyappan-s030-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[வேல — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
You insects of Pazhuthar! Worms!... You trip the tiger—beware;... say 'hm' and it's prison; ask 'why' and it's exile!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s030-u021 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He leaves quickly.]*

---

## Archival scene 31

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s031-u001 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam and Sukhadev are present.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s031-u002 | source:PDF 66 / printed 64 | record:ammaiyappan-s031-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Vedalam! As you told me, I have finished all the arrangements. I have spent two thousand gold coins... jewels... clothes, this and that... bought everything... Will my Muthayi come?... Vedalam... this Kannagi Mansion is waiting for my beloved... will that maiden come?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s031-u003 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s031-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
(Holding them apart) Certainly, brother!... Touch one of the two! (He touches one.)

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s031-u004 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s031-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Ah!... She will definitely come, brother...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s031-u005 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s031-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Will she come?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s031-u006 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s031-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
All right!... Name a flower, brother!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s031-u007 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s031-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
A flower!... This one? Tamarind blossom...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s031-u008 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s031-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Why all this fuss!... I will go myself and bring Muthayi... You stay right here... I will be back at once.

---

## Archival scene 32

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s032-u001 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is in prison. His friends stealthily come and free him. He whispers a secret into their ears and leaves. Vengaiyur soldiers who have been watching from hiding spring on him and seize him, then immediately drag him to Velazhagan.]*

---

## Archival scene 33

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u001 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
What?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u002 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**குறும்பன்:**  
Vedalam has come...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u003 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Vedalam?... Tell him to come, tell him to come...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u004 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Come in, Vedalam... I assume you have come with victory...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u005 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Only the beginning of victory, my lord!—our new quarry is caught by a brand-new hunter!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u006 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
What?... what?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u007 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
I will tell you... Sukhadev and Muthayi—their wedding is at Kannagi Mansion in Anandapuram...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u008 | source:PDF 67 / printed 65 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Ah!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u009 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
I arranged it myself...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u010 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
(Angrily) What?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u011 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Come now... I began this plot only to deliver Muthayi to you...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u012 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Explain it so I can understand...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u013 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
You must do exactly as I say... Sukhadev and Muthayi are going to be married... You must send Muthan there immediately... If Muthan goes, Muthayi will run away with him... While the two of them are escaping, we will drive Muthan off and abduct Muthayi alone...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u014 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Good plan... What if Muthan understands our plot?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u015 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Hm... In the intoxication of love he will not think about anything... a horseman comes in.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u016 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
My lord! Muthan escaped from prison... We have caught him and brought him back...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u017 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Where?... Bring him in... Vedalam, hide yourself for a while...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u018 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is dragged in.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u019 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
All right! You may go... hee... hee... hee... Mutha!... Even if you had not escaped from prison, I was already thinking of releasing you myself... good young man!... I have put you through a great deal... I am sorry for that....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u020 | source:PDF 68 / printed 66 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Sir! You have suddenly changed... How did you realize that I am without refuge?...... Even a god could not have come and told you!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u021 | source:PDF 68→69 / printed 66→67 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
A human being has something called conscience. When a man becomes a beast, that conscience turns into a worm and lies subdued. When beastliness is defeated, the wormlike conscience rises as a spotted peacock and dances... It is in that dance that the beauty of truth begins to shine.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u022 | source:PDF 69 / printed 67 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah! A splendid thought sprouts even here, like a red lotus sprouting from mud...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u023 | source:PDF 69 / printed 67 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Mutha! Do you know what news opened my eyes?... That Sukhadev is going to force your beloved Muthayi into marriage...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u024 | source:PDF 69 / printed 67 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Really?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u025 | source:PDF 69 / printed 67 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Yes! The wedding is at Kannagi Mansion in Anandapuram... Run before her life leaves her... Mutha! You may wonder why I suddenly care so much... You have read the story of Layla-Majnun, have you not?... There is not much difference between this and the episode in which Layla's husband sent her back to her lover Qays... Mutha, let me tell you one thing... you and Muthayi may live as a family under my protection... but why worry about that now?...... Go at once... save your life-companion... [goes inside and returns with fine clothes] Here! Run this instant... regain your lost love... here are fine clothes. Put them on... go and complete your wedding happily!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u026 | source:PDF 69 / printed 67 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I am deeply grateful to you, sir...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u027 | source:PDF 69 / printed 67 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He rushes away. Then he hears laughter and watches from hiding.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u028 | source:PDF 69 / printed 67 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Bravo, my lord! You played it very well.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u029 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
The fellow was thoroughly fooled...

Vedalam!... What is the next arrangement?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u030 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
The next arrangement!... Send five or six horsemen under my command. I will return with that beauty.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u031 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
I will give you a thousand gold coins... if you come back with her...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s033-u032 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s033-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
[To himself] They are plotting.... I will turn that plot to my advantage.

---

## Archival scene 34

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s034-u001 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s034-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Comrades! Thanapathi is using me as a tool to abduct Muthayi. I will use this opportunity and disappear.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s034-u002 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s034-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
Then what will become of us, anna?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s034-u003 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s034-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
You too, somehow escape from here and come to me. Then all of us together will work for Pazhuthar's liberation. Vāṭṭamā nāṉ?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s034-u004 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s034-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன்:**  
Go carefully, anna! We will somehow come and join you.—We must get out by cunning.

---

## Archival scene 35

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u001 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Father, why is no one here? Where is he, father?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u002 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
Oh, oh! In such a hurry already? Once the leaf is laid out, would they fail to serve the rice? Come, girl, come......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u003 | source:PDF 70 / printed 68 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Yes... all the arrangements for the wedding...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u004 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Are you looking around thinking there are no drums, no wedding pavilion, no feast?... This is only the bride's house. The muhurtham will take place elsewhere. Besides... this wedding has to happen without Sukhadev knowing about it...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u005 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Yes.... yes... that Sukhadev...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u006 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tirisangu sees Sukhadev arriving.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u007 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[திரிசங்கு — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
You say he is very handsome—someone you like very much......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u008 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Father... what are you babbling about!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u009 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She turns, sees Sukhadev and faints.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u010 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
What?... what?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u011 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Nothing... nothing... The shock of seeing you made her faint... the heart pounds from happiness, does it not, brother?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u012 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Yes!... yes! Mine too is pounding just like that now...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u013 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
All right; you go upstairs. I will come later.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u014 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev leaves. Muthayi recovers from her faint and rises. Tirisangu shows her costly jewels and clothes.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u015 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Muthayi! Look at these... ornaments set with pearls, coral, gems and diamonds! Shining silk!... glittering diamond! Precious gem! Ah... ha... all these....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u016 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
A price that pleases your heart! A net cast over your daughter!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u017 | source:PDF 71 / printed 69 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Listen to what I say...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u018 | source:PDF 71→72 / printed 69→70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Do not say anything. I understand everything now. The very shell brings the pearl and drops it in the mud!... The red-lotus creeper itself plucks off its petals and gives them to the buffalo! Father, would you plot like this against your own daughter?!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u019 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Daughter!... If you were a daughter, would you let a father's heart burn like this? If you were truly a daughter—an aged father—a loving father—a man who has suffered many days in poverty—now thinks he might finally live a little, would you not think of helping him?... To let me live, am I asking you to jump into a fire? Am I asking for some great sacrifice?.... A position impossible to find... he says he will place you there and worship you as its goddess... yet you sob, heave and lament, foolish girl! I have fulfilled so many wishes of yours; can you not fulfil this first and last wish of mine?....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u020 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Many times I have given you tender coconuts; what difference is there, father, between what you are saying and a coconut tree saying, 'Just once, I will fall on your head'?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u021 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Tch, insolent chatterbox! Keep quiet! In the morning you and the prince are to be married!... Get yourself ready for it...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u022 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He leaves quickly. As he comes outside, he sees Vedalam.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u023 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Come... you have arrived at a good time...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u024 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
What, Tirisangu? How are all my arrangements......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u025 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
All the arrangements are fine! There is just one flaw.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u026 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
What is it?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u027 | source:PDF 72 / printed 70 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Muthayi refuses to marry Sukhadev...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u028 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
That's a fine thing! It is like saying the meal is ready but there is no rice! What, man... earlier you said the two of them were lovers united as one...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u029 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Muthayi is a stubborn girl. I cannot change her obstinacy... You must somehow...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u030 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Hm... I will try... Where is she now?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u031 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
She is inside.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u032 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
All right, all of you come; I will set her right myself!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u033 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam goes inside. Seeing Muthayi, he speaks.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u034 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Foolish girl! Get up! Why are you sitting like this?... Won't you speak?... Hm... this is exactly the time that is slipping away... the sea is roaring... and you are sitting on a catamaran singing an oppari!... Ah! Look to what must be done, dear girl! Crying cannot ward off danger...... Why are you crying?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u035 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
The town laughs; I cry...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u036 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
True. Yet, tender-hearted maiden! Realize that there is still a chance to escape this turmoil...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u037 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
A chance to escape!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u038 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Yes... a chance indeed... your liberation... the sign that your suffering is departing has come in the form of Vedalam...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u039 | source:PDF 73 / printed 71 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d033 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What are you saying?....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u040 | source:PDF 73→74 / printed 71→72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d034 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
You do not know how affectionately Muthan and I have lived as friends... the gold you gave, Sumathi swallowed and kept... Muthan was locked in the camp prison... I gave the wealth I had saved with great hardship over many days and have now freed him.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u041 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d035 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah!... Has my Muthan been freed?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u042 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d036 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Alas, pitiable girl!... Hear more happy news. I came to know that your father is cruel and arranged this marriage by deceiving you... so I ran here at once to save you!... I have come like a wedding guest, trying to free my friend's beloved.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u043 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d037 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Sir! May you live for many years!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u044 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d038 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Why should I live long in this unjust world, girl? Never mind that... Listen, young bird!... Muthan will come here tonight; leave with him.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u045 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d039 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Will he come?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u046 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d040 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Would he fail to come?... Be careful, both of you!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u047 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam leaves. Muthan enters the room disguised as an old man.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u048 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d041 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Aththan! Vedalam said you would come.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u049 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d042 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Vedalam! He is a venomous snake! Muthayi, somehow you must make Sukhadev come here.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u050 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d043 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Sukhadev? Why?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u051 | source:PDF 74 / printed 72 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d044 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Do you know why? For us to leave now is no ordinary matter; danger is waiting all around us!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u052 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tirisangu comes in. Muthan hides.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u053 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d045 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Muthayi! Still not asleep?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u054 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d046 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I cannot sleep, father!... I am deep in thought.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u055 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d047 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
What are you thinking about, girl?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u056 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d048 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
That Vedalam came and put sense into me... all these days I had lost my wits and trusted that Muthan...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u057 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d049 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
My daughter! Is it really you speaking?... Ah! Vedalam was right... Muthayi, only now do I feel I have gained the benefit of having you as my daughter...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u058 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d050 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Father, will you do me a favour?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u059 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d051 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
I will do a thousand; tell me!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u060 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d052 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
In the morning Sukhadev and I are to be married. Now I want to meet him and speak about a few things! My heart too is longing to see him...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u061 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d053 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
My darling daughter!... Is that such a great matter? I will send him right now...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u062 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tirisangu leaves quickly. Muthan tells Muthayi the secret plan and leaves.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u063 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Tirisangu goes to Sukhadev and tells him of Muthayi's eagerness.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u064 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d054 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Ah! My goddess of love! Here I come. Can fire and cotton remain still when brought close together? Uncle! You can go and sleep soundly—I will not let Muthayi sleep tonight—I am going to keep talking to her!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u065 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev comes to where Muthayi is.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u066 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d055 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
My dear! Why did you call me? Oh! Won't you speak? Are you this shy?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u067 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d056 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I need to discuss something.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u068 | source:PDF 75 / printed 73 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d057 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Discuss something? A lover and beloved—this bedchamber is a pavilion of sensual delight, not a place for discussing news, my dear!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u069 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d058 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
You must not make all these jokes...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u070 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d059 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
What do you want to talk about? Speak!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u071 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d060 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What is your opinion about equality between men and women?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u072 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d061 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Half man—half woman—my principle is that men and women should be created as Ardhanarishvaras themselves—if I were God, that is exactly how I would make them!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u073 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d062 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah... you have stated a vast and complicated matter so briefly!... The woman fortunate enough to attain you is lucky indeed—I want to ask one more thing.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u074 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d063 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Do not ask all these things retail—ask wholesale!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u075 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d064 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
If I ask it, you cannot answer immediately. We have to talk for a long time.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u076 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d065 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
We will talk—never mind—what other pleasure do I have except talking with you!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u077 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d066 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What a lovely moon—shall the two of us go toward the riverbank and talk of pleasant stories?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u078 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d067 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Ah!... You have said the long thing—come, let us set out, my dear!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u079 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d068 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Shh... do not touch... all that is for tomorrow! Come, let us go...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u080 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d069 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
I am ready...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u081 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d070 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
You say men and women are equal—then you in women's clothes, and I in men's...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u082 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d071 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Oh no, in women's clothes?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u083 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d072 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
See! Being a woman means degradation...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u084 | source:PDF 76 / printed 74 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d073 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
No... no... it is not that; I would feel shy if the men saw me...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u085 | source:PDF 76→77 / printed 74→75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d074 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
We are going secretly so no one will know, are we not? When you and I are one life in two bodies, what is wrong in your wearing my clothes and my wearing yours?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u086 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d075 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
There is nothing wrong, my golden treasure! I will do exactly as you say...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u087 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev appears in women's clothes and Muthayi in men's clothes. Velazhagan's soldiers are hidden nearby.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u088 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d076 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
My jewel, Muthayi! What a sweet moment!... What a wonderful sight!... Ah! To have you beside me... alas, I do not even understand where we are going!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u089 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d077 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
You will not understand... you will not!... You... must come without speaking.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u090 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The soldiers mistake Sukhadev for Muthayi, attack him and carry him away. Muthayi remains. Muthan comes up from behind and startles her.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u091 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d078 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Hey! Who is that?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u092 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d079 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u093 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They both laugh.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u094 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d080 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
So—that is done. What is our next plan?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u095 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d081 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
The next plan? Give me your ring like this... up... put it on the finger!... Here!... stretch out your finger...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u096 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He puts his ring on her finger.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u097 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d082 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What is this?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u098 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d083 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
When two lovers are of one mind, exchanging rings itself is marriage...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u099 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d084 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
All right... we are married now... what next?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u100 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d085 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
From now on... f... f... we have to run a family...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u101 | source:PDF 77 / printed 75 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d086 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Run a family right here in the forest?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u102 | source:PDF 77→78 / printed 75→76 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d087 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Why not?... Did Rama and Sita not live in the forest for fourteen years? When a man and woman are alone, there is no dwelling more delightful than forest-dwelling... Look at the peacock dancing in joy! Look at the cuckoo singing melody to the ear's fill! Look at the river!—look at the waterfall!—look at its beauty!—come a little nearer and look!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u103 | source:PDF 78 / printed 76 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d088 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Hm...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u104 | source:PDF 78 / printed 76 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d089 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Rama must have spoken to Sita like that... Would forest life have been bitter for them?... It may have been bitter for Lakshmana... poor man, married yet a brahmachari!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u105 | source:PDF 78 / printed 76 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d090 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Mischief!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s035-u106 | source:PDF 78 / printed 76 | record:ammaiyappan-s035-d091 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
All right, Muthayi, come! We must live hidden in disguise... otherwise Velazhagan will somehow catch us... come... come!...

---

## Archival scene 36

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u001 | source:PDF 78 / printed 76 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Muthayi! Am I the only one waiting for you?... Look, even the moonlight in that sky is waiting... Angry girl! Maiden of cool speech! Why have you still not come?... Are those horsemen blockheads?... Or are these steeds themselves unable to understand my urgency? How long... how long... I told them then itself... that a shortcut should be laid between Anandapuram and the capital... If this foolish king had listened, how quickly my Muthayi could have arrived by now!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u002 | source:PDF 78 / printed 76 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The soldiers bring Sukhadev dressed as a woman.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u003 | source:PDF 78 / printed 76 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனபதி:**  
Muthayi! You've come?... Muthayi, won't you speak?... Ah! Who has closed those lips that pour out golden words?... These horsemen are fools!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u004 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The gag is removed. Sukhadev cries out, and Velazhagan grows angry.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u005 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Sir, my lord! Don't hit me...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u006 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
How did you come here?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u007 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Muthayi and I—she as a woman and I as a man... no... no... she as a man... I as a woman... we put on disguises and went to the riverbank to play happily... and then, my lord, this danger befell me!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u008 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
Fool! Get out of here without telling anyone outside...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u009 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[A proclamation is beaten out on the drum. Muthan and Muthayi enter in disguise.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u010 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பறையடிப்பவன்:**  
This is to inform everyone that... a man named Muthan has escaped from the Vengai-country army... His identifying marks are—a budding moustache—a handsome young man—a ruddy complexion—a bronze voice—whoever catches and hands him over will receive a proper reward!... This is the order of Velazhagar, commander of Vengai country!... Dum!... dum!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u011 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan and Muthayi come toward a merchant's house. The merchant is with a child.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u012 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வணி:**  
See that, little one! Dum... duma... duma... will you catch the runaway and hand him over?...... [The child cries.] Hey!... don't cry... lovely sight... sacred ornament of a beautiful gem-studded mansion... an indelible painting... a dancing golden lamp... aararo...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u013 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan and Muthayi are seen approaching.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u014 | source:PDF 79 / printed 77 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
Who? Who... are you? What do you want? I can't give you any alms... I have an infant to care for...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u015 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
We're looking for some kind of work...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u016 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
Have brother and younger sister set out together?.......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u017 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Oh no!... we're husband and wife, master...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u018 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
Hmm... what work can you do?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u019 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
We'll do whatever work we're given without refusing. He isn't well... only I...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u020 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
All right... can you raise this child?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u021 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Oh!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u022 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
Do you have experience?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u023 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Even the child is reaching for me... come here, little one!......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u024 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
The child has no mother—has come into the world, as the source puts it, like a banana bunch... you must look after it... there are no wages... just food for the two of you... here is a thatched hut... you can live in it...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u025 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
All right...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u026 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
What's your name?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u027 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
My name is Ponni... and he...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s036-u028 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s036-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Mm... don't say your husband's name... poor thing... my name is Ponnan, master!

---

## Archival scene 37

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u001 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Hmm... two young birds—blossoming flowers—where have you gone to sing your love-song? Tirisangu! Don't worry. You have found a Manmatha fit for your daughter. Now let her sing her song—and him his!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u002 | source:PDF 80 / printed 78 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரிசங்கு:**  
It was not because he was a Manmatha that I got tangled up in all this trouble and arranged this marriage. I, who lie in a leaky hut......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u003 | source:PDF 80→81 / printed 78→79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
You thought you could stroll on an upper storey as the Palayakkarar's relative by marriage—that is it, isn't it! What of it now—Sukhadev and Muthayi will soon return after finishing their romantic tour.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u004 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
When? When I am on my way to the cremation ground?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u005 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
So what if it is then? The grandson who will light your funeral pyre will be a future Palayakkarar, won't he? Hee... hee... hee... Tirisangu! Leave all that. I gave you the idea for such a magnificent wedding arrangement—where is my reward?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u006 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Reward?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u007 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Shall I call it wages?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u008 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Do not play with my pain.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u009 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Play? This Vedalam does not play, Tirisangu... The reward you owe me must come, respectfully. Otherwise......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u010 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Otherwise what will you do?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u011 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
What will I do? I will tell the Palayakkarar that you deceived Sukhadev and ruined him. Be careful!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u012 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Do not frighten me. The Palayakkarar is not a fool who will simply believe your unsupported lie.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u013 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
No proof? The fact that Muthayi is not in your house is itself strong proof for me, do you understand?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u014 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Enough! Stop! You were the one who gave the idea too.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u015 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
That will not help you in this case, man! Wait... wait, I will deal with you. If you behave properly, you will survive. Otherwise.....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s037-u016 | source:PDF 81 / printed 79 | record:ammaiyappan-s037-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**திரி:**  
Otherwise—I will die. That is all, isn't it! Do not threaten me. Do whatever you can.

---

## Archival scene 38

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u001 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record:ammaiyappan-s038-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பலதேவர்:**  
Tirisangu! That fellow! He acted like a good man and carried out a jackal's trick—the traitor!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u002 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record:ammaiyappan-s038-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
It is the good people one must not trust in these times, my lord.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u003 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record:ammaiyappan-s038-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Who's there? (A servant enters.) Go at once, bind Tirisangu hand and foot, and drag him here. No matter how much wealth is lost—only bring my son back to me. If he does not return, the queen will die.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u004 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record:ammaiyappan-s038-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
No one should learn that Sukhadev is missing. I myself will find him by stratagem and bring him back to you.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u005 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record:ammaiyappan-s038-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Thank you very much. I will give you five hundred gold coins—if Sukhadev is found, I will give you five hundred gold coins as a reward. Wait here—I will be back.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u006 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Baladevar leaves.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u007 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record:ammaiyappan-s038-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
They say sorrow comes with company and happiness comes alone—that is wrong! Is happiness not marching upon Vedalam like a line of ants? Hmm... one man can seek comfort only by striking another! The fly is food for the spider—the spider is food for the lizard—the lizard is food for the cat—the cat is food for the Kuravan—the Kuravan is food for Yama—that is my path too! At the speed of my rise, perhaps I may even become the Palayakkarar of Pazhuthar!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u008 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record:ammaiyappan-s038-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
(Coming back from inside) Vedalam! Here, take this!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s038-u009 | source:PDF 82 / printed 80 | record:ammaiyappan-s038-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Most honoured lord! I will somehow bring your precious son back. May the Lord be my aid! Ammaiyappa!

---

## Archival scene 39

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u001 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is talking with his friends. Muthayi stands at a crack in the doorway and watches.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u002 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Pazhuthar must win freedom somehow... This thought must take root in the people's hearts... If the feeling of liberation spreads like wildfire, then in a single day we can raise the flag of freedom in Pazhuthar and drive out Vengai country's domination!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u003 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தோழன் 1:**  
If we are to change the people's minds...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u004 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தோழன் 2:**  
Thinking alone will not do... we must act...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u005 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தோழன் 3:**  
Yes... that is my opinion too...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u006 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Wherever the people direct their attention, we must spread our ideas through every such sphere...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u007 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தோழ 2:**  
Ah!... what anna says is right!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u008 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
You go... I will come after attending to the rest...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u009 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They leave. Muthayi enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u010 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Muthayi...!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u011 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Yes; what is all this?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u012 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
The beginning of the liberation struggle! They are my comrades... I am gathering an entire force like this so that Pazhuthar may be liberated...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u013 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
I am afraid...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u014 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Foolish girl! Afraid?... Purananuru will laugh at you!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u015 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
If you wage a liberation struggle and your life is put in danger...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u016 | source:PDF 83 / printed 81 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What else—will they lay a feast for a freedom fighter?... Look here... even this child keeps looking at me... Hey!... if only we had one like this....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u017 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Wait another eight or nine months...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s039-u018 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s039-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah... is that so? You did not tell me...

---

## Archival scene 40

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u001 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record: | occurrence:ammaiyappan-song-004 | origin: -->
*Japa / performance cue*  
> [Young ascetic Sukhadev sits in nishta, performing japa of “Muthayi.” Vedalam enters disguised as an ascetic.]

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u002 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Om Shakti! Chamundi, Mahakali, Parameswari, Renukamba, Sundari, Soundari, Niranthari, Duranthari, O Uma who stands as radiance!.... You remove the suffering of those who behold and worship sukha varttiyinai!... Ambike! Jagadambike! Kali!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u003 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He looks at Sukhadev.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u004 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d002 | occurrence:ammaiyappan-song-004 | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Muthayi... Muthayi...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u005 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Young ascetic! First, my greetings...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u006 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
White-bearded elder!... The world is false! What do you want?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u007 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
In this false world, a true jnani!... What place are you from?... Looking at you, it seems as though you have lost something that captured your heart!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u008 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Ha... ha... ha! You are an extraordinary man! How did you see what was in my heart? How did you reveal it? Are you the sage who knows the three times—Devarishi Narada himself?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u009 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Brother!... My eye of wisdom tells me that you are a man defeated in love... Is that true?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u010 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Yes! How do you know?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u011 | source:PDF 84 / printed 82 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
For the virtuous, every corner of the world is visible... You are a palace-dweller!... Through the enchantment of a woman, you have become an ashram-dweller! Isn't that so?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u012 | source:PDF 85 / printed 83 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Yes, Swami! They say only those who have escaped the enchantment of women can become ashram-dwellers... I myself have been bound by that enchantment and become a mendicant!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u013 | source:PDF 85 / printed 83 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Nothing surprising... Viswamitra left the palace and came to the ashram... Even there, the trouble from beauties did not leave him... he, a very summit of the greatness of penance... the child Menaka bore...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u014 | source:PDF 85 / printed 83 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Ah! Most excellent ascetic, rich in ripened experience and abundant devotion! Through you alone I seek the end I desire. Grant me the boon...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u015 | source:PDF 85 / printed 83 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
I understand! You want to attain that four-letter maiden... that is all, isn't it?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u016 | source:PDF 85 / printed 83 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Mu—th—tha—yi! Ah!... taccu... taccu... You said it exactly right, true guru!... That jewel of a woman's name has only four letters!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u017 | source:PDF 85 / printed 83 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
I will arrange your marriage with her... You do not need this guise at such a young age!... Do all that must be done in worldly life first, and in the years when age no longer lets you go on, say ‘Siva, Siva’—that is the proper course for human beings...
