<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u018 | source:PDF 85 / printed 83 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Will I get my Muthayi?... My god who came to me without my seeking!... Just as Narada came between Murugan and Valli, you have come between Muthayi and me—my father blessed with the Lord's divine grace! Praise!... praise!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s040-u019 | source:PDF 85 / printed 83 | record:ammaiyappan-s040-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
All right, come... let us go to my place... You will have Muthayi soon...

---

## Archival scene 41

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s041-u001 | source:PDF 85→86 / printed 83→84 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi takes the merchant's child to the seashore. A storm rises. Heavy rain pours down. She comes running with the child!... A swaying tree breaks. Muthayi is trapped beneath the fallen tree. The child is separated and left alone.]*

---

## Archival scene 42

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u001 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The storm has subsided. Vedalam and Sukhadev are seated in a mandapam.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u002 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record:ammaiyappan-s042-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதாளம்:**  
Some phantom came upon the woman, seized her to devour her... casting a spell over her eyes...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u003 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The merchant enters.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u004 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record:ammaiyappan-s042-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வணிகர்:**  
Sir... sir! Did you see a woman and a child, sir?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u005 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record:ammaiyappan-s042-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
What! A woman and a child?... There are so many men and so many women on the seashore...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u006 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record:ammaiyappan-s042-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வணி:**  
No, sir! As usual, the maid came to the seashore carrying my child...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u007 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record:ammaiyappan-s042-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
Go... go... go and search!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u008 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The merchant leaves.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s042-u009 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record:ammaiyappan-s042-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Seems what we're searching for itself can't be found. And now this fellow too... Come, swami, let's go.

---

## Archival scene 43

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s043-u001 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The merchant comes searching for the child. He finds the child standing beneath a tree and crying, and picks the child up.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s043-u002 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record:ammaiyappan-s043-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
God!... You saved me. You saved this poor man...

---

## Archival scene 44

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s044-u001 | source:PDF 86 / printed 84 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi lies unconscious beneath the tree. Sumathi comes that way and sees her.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s044-u002 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s044-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுமதி:**  
Who is that?... Alas, poor thing! [She looks.] Ah!... Muthayi... Muthayi...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s044-u003 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She supports Muthayi in her arms and leads her away.]*

---

## Archival scene 45

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s045-u001 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s045-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Where is Ponni?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s045-u002 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s045-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தனவணிகர்:**  
If I had not gone, that would have been the end of the child. A tree branch broke in the wind and fell...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s045-u003 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s045-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Where is Ponni?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s045-u004 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s045-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தன:**  
Oh! In my haste I forgot her!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s045-u005 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s045-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
You forgot her! It is not only poor people like us; your class of lords has long since forgotten one virtue of the compassionate heart.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s045-u006 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan runs out.]*

---

## Archival scene 46

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s046-u001 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi enters Sumathi's house with her. Vedalam and Sukhadev see them.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s046-u002 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s046-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
They are going into this house... Swami, shall we go too?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s046-u003 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s046-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Wait, my son!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s046-u004 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s046-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
I'm waiting...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s046-u005 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s046-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Come... come... let us go back to our place... We now know where Muthayi is. It is my duty now to unite her with the man she ought to join...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s046-u006 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s046-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Fulfil that duty quickly, Swami!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s046-u007 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s046-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
So be it... all right... all right... come...

---

## Archival scene 47

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s047-u001 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s047-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
That day, without understanding you, I spoke angrily. Forgive me...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s047-u002 | source:PDF 87 / printed 85 | record:ammaiyappan-s047-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சும:**  
Let us forget all that!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s047-u003 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s047-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
After your marriage, where did all your mischievous talk run off and hide?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s047-u004 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s047-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சும:**  
It has not hidden anywhere... Let him regain his health... then listen to me talk! Muthayi, you call my speech kurumbu—mischief; do you know what he calls it?... karumbu—sugarcane!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s047-u005 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s047-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Hmm, all right, all right... do not keep talking only about your man... pay a little attention to mine too...

---

## Archival scene 48

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s048-u001 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev knocks at the door. Inside, Vedalam is talking with the commander.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s048-u002 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s048-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Swami! Swami!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s048-u003 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s048-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Wait. My nishta is not over yet.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s048-u004 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s048-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Sukhadev has come, Swami! Open the door.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s048-u005 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s048-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Blind devotee! Wait. I am talking with God—about Muthayi...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s048-u006 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s048-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
(To himself) Aha! Is he talking with God? We cannot see Bhagavan ourselves. Sinful soul that I am! At least while the Swami speaks, let me hide and watch!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s048-u007 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He opens the window shutter and looks inside. He is badly startled.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s048-u008 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s048-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Ah! Ayyo! The commander!... Vedalam is climbing the drumstick tree again. Appa! Good thing—I escaped... (He runs.)

---

## Archival scene 49

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s049-u001 | source:PDF 88 / printed 86 | record:ammaiyappan-s049-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
It seems the escaped Muthan too is gathering a liberation force and plotting to free Pazhuthar. Like two mangoes with one stone, we must catch that fellow Muthan as well.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s049-u002 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s049-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**தள:**  
Let that be, Vedalam! Why talk pointlessly to me now about a liberation force when I am wandering in search of where my pleasure lies? First bring that beautiful-bodied woman somehow!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s049-u003 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s049-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Wait. I will return victorious.

---

## Archival scene 50

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u001 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi is lying down. Vedalam calls out.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u002 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Amma!... Amma!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u003 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Who are you, sir?... What do you want?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u004 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Who here is Muthayi?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u005 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Why!... What is it?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u006 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Muthan has been attacked by enemies and is in a dying condition.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u007 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah!... Aththan!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u008 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
Are you Muthayi? If you come with me, you can see him alive...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u009 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Sir!... Where is he? Take me to him...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u010 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வே:**  
All right, come; I will show you.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u011 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev arrives there and sees Muthayi leaving with Vedalam.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s050-u012 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s050-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[சுகதேவ் — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Aha! Is this villain the one taking my beloved away? Unarmed as I am, how can I take direct action?... Hmm... we shall see...

---

## Archival scene 51

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s051-u001 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record:ammaiyappan-s051-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
I abandoned my beloved in pitch darkness and am crying out. How will I rescue you... my precious beloved! He came in the guise of a holy man and tricked me! Muthayi! Muthayi! How will I rescue you from that brute?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s051-u002 | source:PDF 89 / printed 87 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan passes that way.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s051-u003 | source:PDF 89→90 / printed 87→88 | record:ammaiyappan-s051-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Hey, Mutha! Stop—stop—you came at just the right time! There is no time now to ask where you are, how you are, and all that... Vedalam, dressed as an ascetic, has deceived my dearest beloved Muthayi and is taking her somewhere.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s051-u004 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s051-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah! Where?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s051-u005 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s051-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
He must have gone to his rest-house...

---

## Archival scene 52

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u001 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Inside, a figure is covered with a blanket... Vedalam arrives with Muthayi.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u002 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
There... your beloved!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u003 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Eagerly crying 'Aththan,' she pulls away the blanket. Velazhagan rises.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u004 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
...Ah!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u005 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேலழகன்:**  
Muthayi!... Full moon!... you've fallen into my hands?... You called 'Aththan'; that is not dear Muthan, but Commander Velazhagan! Do you recognize me?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u006 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Commander Velazhagan! You, who ought to protect the country, have become a wanderer like this... No. I am your sister...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u007 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Only fools would believe that the flower and the bee are brother and sister, dear!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u008 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He grabs her hand.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u009 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Fie!... Let go of me! Shameless man!... Poisonous storm! I am not a tree that will topple in the cyclone of your lust...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u010 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Scold me! Scold me well! Every letter tastes sweet... A caged parrot cries not from anger!... from fear! Even that will become normal with habit...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u011 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Don't come near me!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u012 | source:PDF 90 / printed 88 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
The commander of this country's army calls you... Do you know what happens to those who do not obey my command?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u013 | source:PDF 91 / printed 89 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
You'll kill me! That's all, isn't it?... I won't fear death! Is death more terrible than you? No... no... never!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u014 | source:PDF 91 / printed 89 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Muthayi!—No flower has ever escaped from my iron-like arms!... Remember that!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u015 | source:PDF 91 / printed 89 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Let me go, you wrongdoer!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u016 | source:PDF 91 / printed 89 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
The whole world turns on the axle called injustice!... Muthayi!... You can no longer escape... Yield of your own accord!... [He comes closer—Muthayi tries to run.] Where are you running? Wherever you go, I will not let you go... If you fly as a skylark, I will become the hawk circling you!... If you leap as a spotted deer, I will roar as a tiger! Even if at last you become a corpse, I will become the sandalwood log that touches your body and burns with it! My delight!... Yield... fragrant new blossom flowering in spring! Come to me. Coloured lotus! Bouquet of dazzling flowers!... I will sweep you into my powerful arms... Leap upon me, green parrot!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u017 | source:PDF 91 / printed 89 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Do not ruin yourself in vain; stand away...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u018 | source:PDF 91 / printed 89 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
[With a terrifying laugh.] Now no one will come forward to offer you refuge by destroying me!... You've read in stories: the beloved is in danger... the hero runs in and saves her! None of that will happen here!... Even that Sita could not be easily rescued from Ashoka Vanam by the divine-incarnate Ramachandra Murthi! Remember! Pitiful woman!... Someone has taught you that you must conduct yourself with chastity like Kannagi!... That was the age of the Silappathikaram... literature that ought to be banned... a philosophy this army commander cannot permit... O maiden! Come near...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u019 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Warrior who bears spear and sword in battle! Do not behave so cowardly toward a woman...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u020 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Shall I behave heroically then?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u021 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Let me go...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u022 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Even fruit that comes to the hand—a bird that comes within sight—people will not let go! Muthayi! I warn you; don't test my patience......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u023 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He comes near to embrace her.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u024 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Fie! [She slaps him.]

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u025 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Ah!....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u026 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He draws his sword. Muthan jumps down from above.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u027 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Mutha!... Don't die in vain! Think carefully about who your enemy is...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u028 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Me? I am the warrior who was trapped and ruined in the `kāppāri` of your arbitrary rule—in the hall of dictatorship!... But today that warrior has not come... I have come to reward you for the blood I shed—for the tortures I endured—I have come to reward you, man, reward you! Madman who hunts women! Liquor and the pleasures of women may hide in the shadow of your delusion... but you are intoxicated madmen who cannot realize that a revolutionary force will surge up and smash that delusion to dust...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u029 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Don't forget whom you're talking to, Mutha!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u030 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I am playing with you, who are playing with my life!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u031 | source:PDF 92 / printed 90 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
If I move my forefinger, you'll be smashed to pieces...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u032 | source:PDF 92→93 / printed 90→91 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ha, ha!... Will your pointing finger shoot me?—No... smash me to pieces... The age in which you lived by intimidation is over, Commander! Now an age of rationalism is coming, making room for people of character alone to live. You are my enemy. Keep your longbow... The seats of power of those who, like you, sped along in the footprints of dictatorship have become hollows and mounds, then graveyards with no trace of where they once stood!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u033 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Do you know what is in my hand?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u034 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
I know very well... What is the glitter of a knife in a coward's hand before the roughness of a warrior's palm? You have a knife!... Here is my weapon! A weapon forged in the camp of Duty, Dignity and Discipline! A weapon that stands against scoundrels—knife-thrusts—dishonourable acts!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u035 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேல:**  
Such a great warrior?... A warrior who will face a knife bare-handed?... Here, let us see that too...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u036 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The two fight fiercely. Kurumban arrives. Seeing the situation, he runs away. The fight ends with the commander's death.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u037 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Aththan!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u038 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Muthayi!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u039 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Kurumban arrives with soldiers.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u040 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record:ammaiyappan-s052-d033 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**குறும்பன்:**  
Ah!... Commander!... Ayyo, are you dead? Hey, carry him away. Hey Mutha! You killed him, didn't you?... Hey, arrest this fellow too.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s052-u041 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The soldiers fight Muthan. In the end Muthan is captured... Muthayi cries out and faints.]*

---

## Archival scene 53

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u001 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They carry away the commander's corpse. Sukhadev sees it.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u002 | source:PDF 93 / printed 91 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Ah!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u003 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is dragged in.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u004 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Hey, Mutha! You killed the commander?... Bravo, bravo... thank you... thank you... Where is Vedalam? Didn't he die?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u005 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[He goes inside.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u006 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Ah!... Muthayi!... Muthayi!... Open your eyes and look... the warrior Sukhadev has come... Open your eyes and look, Muthayi! Get up...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u007 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
[Coming out of her faint.] Are you the grove criminal?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u008 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
I am no criminal... that Muthan is the criminal!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u009 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ayyo, my god of love! In your hands...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u010 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
It's nothing... only a water cup, dear... Don't be afraid, dear... get up...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u011 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Sinner! So you alone have come?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u012 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Why are you angry, Muthayi? Is this some lovers' sulking?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u013 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
What are you saying now?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u014 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
What else would I say? About our marriage, of course!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u015 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
You scoundrel!... You have come to speak a heroic epic to me while I stand at the edge of agony? Is this the lesson they taught you in your palace? Go... get out! Hmm, are you going or not?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u016 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Uh-uh... no one is going anywhere... Muthayi, do you know who I am? The son of the future emperor!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u017 | source:PDF 94 / printed 92 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
The emperor's son! Hmm... you have no eyes... no human feeling... They say a woman's reproach is terrible. Haven't you read that?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u018 | source:PDF 95 / printed 93 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Hmm... why would I read all that... Muthayi, now you simply must marry me... dear Muthayi!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u019 | source:PDF 95 / printed 93 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Hmm... I am ready... It is this body you want, isn't it?... You were bewitched by the sheen of my body, weren't you?..... Hmm... feast your eyes on my beauty! Someone said: only the body was soiled; the heart was not. Not so with me... I am ready to lay my chastity at your feet... You donkey charging in lust! Rake my heart with your cruel claws!..... Hmm... don't delay! Plunder my life!... Shatter my resolve to pieces! Representative of fiends who think of women as objects of prostitution!... Why should I hesitate? Here I am ready! My corpse is speaking to you! My dead body calls you! Come... come... deceiver! Here I am—Muthayi—Muthan's wife—your `vellatti`!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s053-u020 | source:PDF 95 / printed 93 | record:ammaiyappan-s053-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
What? You are Muthan's wife?... Muthayi, forgive me!... Listening to your father's words, I became corrupted like this! Trusting household life, I went as far as the boundary of ascetic life! Muthayi! I had carried my love for you toward marriage. Dear sister! I will go right now and save your beloved.

---

## Archival scene 54

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s054-u001 | source:PDF 95 / printed 93 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Vedalam sits in a ruined mandapam with one thousand gold coins.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s054-u002 | source:PDF 95 / printed 93 | record:ammaiyappan-s054-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வேதா:**  
I got a thousand gold coins! God! A thousand salutations to you... Somehow make me the Palaiyakkaran of Pazhuthar... a crore salutations to you! A thousand gold coins for bringing Muthayi... From now on we don't need this disguise... We are subjects to none; we fear Yama not!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s054-u003 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev arrives there. Enraged, he pushes down a stone from the mandapam and kills Vedalam.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s054-u004 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s054-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
This is the end for the wrongdoer!... In this world, only the man who reforms survives!

---

## Archival scene 55

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s055-u001 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s055-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சக்கரவர்த்தி:**  
Commander Velazhagan... has been killed... a great disgrace to Vengai Nadu! Not only did he desert the army, he also killed the commander... That sinner must not be spared! Hang him at once.

---

## Archival scene 56

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u001 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சாமியார்:**  
For some reason, I have begun to doubt whether Sukhadev will return. Fate can close even our eyes... Even if Sukhadev stood before us, that fate has the terrifying power to draw a curtain over our sight...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u002 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Poongavanam enters as he speaks.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u003 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Hmm... the explanation of fate has begun!... You trust the swami—the poor swami trusts fate—fate lives trusting the swami—if fate keeps turning round like a wheel, Sukhadev too must be wandering somewhere or other...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u004 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சாமி:**  
Hasty women...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u005 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Yes, Swami! That is entirely... entirely true... I am hasty... hasty indeed...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u006 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Poongavanam, enough. Stop it.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u007 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Sukhadev comes running in.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u008 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
Father... father...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u009 | source:PDF 96 / printed 94 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**ராணி:**  
You have come back, my treasure!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u010 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
There is no time to fondle me... Father... matters have gone too far...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u011 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
What are you saying?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u012 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Only if you come can a life be saved!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u013 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
What is it, Sukhadev?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u014 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
A poor man is being taken to the gallows! Save him... They called him an enemy-country spy, charged him with murder, and sentenced him... Father, set out at once for the gate of Vengai Puram!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u015 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Tell me the matter in detail...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u016 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d014 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
They have sentenced Muthan to death by hanging!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u017 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Poongavanam cries out, “Muthaa!” and falls.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u018 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d015 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Aunt!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u019 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d016 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Poongavanam!... Who is there? Go at once and bring Physician Boopathi!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u020 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Physician Boopathi arrives, examines Poongavanam, and gives her medicine.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u021 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d017 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Do not be afraid!... It is only mental shock... nothing else! Prabhu, why did she suddenly faint like this?... Any shock news?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u022 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d018 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Nothing like that... You remember Muthan, Kaveri's son, who used to be the horseman in our mansion? It seems that Muthan has been sentenced to hanging! As soon as she heard that...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u023 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Poongavanam again cries out, “Muthaa!”... Boopathi's mind begins working rapidly.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u024 | source:PDF 97 / printed 95 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d019 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Prabhu! There are stairs behind this mansion, are there not?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u025 | source:PDF 98 / printed 96 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d020 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
There are...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u026 | source:PDF 98 / printed 96 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Boopathi surveys the room carefully. He studies the three paintings—love-pigeons, a cow seeking its calf, and the deer-skin Vengai.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u027 | source:PDF 98 / printed 96 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d021 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூப:**  
Please take me to those rear stairs!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u028 | source:PDF 98 / printed 96 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[They go. Boopathi counts the steps.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u029 | source:PDF 98 / printed 96 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d022 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Aunt... aunt... do not worry, aunt! Good grief... Muthayi must be somewhere there worrying about Muthan too... Aha!... I came at such speed... and this physician has slowed even my speed down... Hmm... he has gone off to count steps—steps!... Come on, sir.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u030 | source:PDF 98 / printed 96 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d023 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
What is it, Boopathi; why did you count the steps?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u031 | source:PDF 98→99 / printed 96→97 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d024 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூப:**  
I counted the steps—and with them I counted an old incident too... Aha! I remember it clearly—as though it were happening now, everything stands before my eyes exactly as a scene! I will tell that scene exactly. Listen carefully... One night this deceitful swami came running in haste and called me. There was a secret—he wanted it hidden. I came! He blindfolded me on the way. I refused. He gave me a sack of gold. I agreed. I did not know where he was taking me. My eyes remained bound. I could tell that I was being led up some steps... I counted the steps carefully. Thirty-six steps! I remember it well... In a dark room, amid dim light, my eyes were untied. To find out what place it was, I looked all around... These same three paintings were there even then!... A woman lay on a cot... her face was thoroughly covered with a black cloth... The woman writhed in labour pain. It seemed she might die in a little while... Then I treated her... Aha!... Aha!... a beautiful child—the swami immediately handed the child to a man named Kaveri. Kaveri ran away with the child. My eyes were bound again and I was brought back home. The very next day my journey abroad began. So that supreme secret lay buried in the underworld. Since I returned, I asked the swami any number of times; he would not tell me. But now I understand that this very room is where that child was born! Yes... this is the place where a cradle should have been hung for Muthan, who today stands at the gallows! But one thing, Prabhu! Why did this swami take so much care? I still do not understand...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u032 | source:PDF 99 / printed 97 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Poongavanam rises.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u033 | source:PDF 99 / printed 97 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d025 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
I will tell you! I am Muthan's mother!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u034 | source:PDF 99 / printed 97 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d026 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Ah!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u035 | source:PDF 99 / printed 97 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d027 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
The one who deceived me is this very fiend here!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u036 | source:PDF 99 / printed 97 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d028 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
What!......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u037 | source:PDF 99→100 / printed 97→98 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d029 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Anna! Forgive me! When you and anni had gone north, I fell into his net! This young ascetic used to come often, saying he would preach devotion... For one day—two days he spoke about the world. Then he explained the noble qualities of the creator of the world. He described even divine incarnations as having engaged in love-play. Maya Kannan—Manmathan—Rathi Devi—Mahesan, Parvati—Valli's bridegroom—Brahma, Tilottama—Menaka, Viswamithan—he told all these mythological tales—the intoxication in his words made my mind reel... Why describe it further—I fell... I fell... I fell from the path of chastity that the Tamil woman guards and protects... He said he would give up sanyasa and begin family life. At last Muthan came into my womb! Giving as his reason, ‘If I marry you, the town will clap its hands and laugh,’ he abandoned me! He is Muthan's father! I am the mother!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u038 | source:PDF 100 / printed 98 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d030 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Oh!... So this is the reason, aunt? Only now do I understand why you said you would not marry the king of Vengai Nadu!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u039 | source:PDF 100 / printed 98 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d031 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூப:**  
Ha... ha... ha!... They say detached ascetics do not seek even revolution—you, however, have sought Poongavanam herself... ha... ha... ha!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u040 | source:PDF 100 / printed 98 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d032 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பல:**  
Boopathi! I will give you five thousand gold coins; do not tell this shameful news outside!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s056-u041 | source:PDF 100 / printed 98 | record:ammaiyappan-s056-d033 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூப:**  
I do not want five thousand gold coins! Because once before I became a slave to gold, this false masquerader was able to keep up his show for all these years!... Honourable Prabhu! There is no use hiding this secret any longer—go at once and save Muthan...

---

## Archival scene 57

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s057-u001 | source:PDF 101 / printed 99 | record:ammaiyappan-s057-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன் 1:**  
Comrades! We must free Muthannan somehow.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s057-u002 | source:PDF 101 / printed 99 | record:ammaiyappan-s057-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன் 2:**  
I am ready to sacrifice even my life to rescue that warrior of ideals...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s057-u003 | source:PDF 101 / printed 99 | record:ammaiyappan-s057-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**வீரன் 1:**  
Even if we have to smash the gallows to dust, we will free Muthannan... Come!

---

## Archival scene 58

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u001 | source:PDF 101 / printed 99 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்காவனம்:**  
Muthayi! Even after hearing all this, why do you say you will not speak to us?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u002 | source:PDF 101 / printed 99 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Do you think my deep sighs have no power to speak?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u003 | source:PDF 101 / printed 99 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
My dear Muthayi!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u004 | source:PDF 101 / printed 99 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Do not touch me!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u005 | source:PDF 101 / printed 99 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Are you the only one who worries about Muthan?... Do none of us worry?... To you he is a husband—to me, a son!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u006 | source:PDF 101→103 / printed 99→101 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Son! Do not say that once more... Your palace honour will be ground into dust. Shameless as you are, how can you call Muthan your son?... What a mother!... A mother who kept her mouth shut when her son was treated like a dog!... A mother who listened with relish to the news that her son was wasting away from hunger!... The very air of your palace drove you to hide the affection called son! Your false honour—status—respect—mannaangatti—everything!—stood in the way of calling him your child! Was it not because no blemish must fall on Palayakkarar pride that you treated the son you carried for ten months and bore as though he were a wanderer? Where is that arrogance now, mother? Have you come searching for your son because the curtain drawn over that arrogance has been torn away?... A mother who protects palace honour! A father who fans religious fanaticism!... The mother played the chaste wife!... The father danced the devotee!... Amma lived like an innocent maiden!... Appan lived out the devotee's evil deed! O Amma-Appars! Creatures of wretched character! New editions of fear itself! The woman who lost her chastity lived as a maiden—the lust-blinded man wandered as a swami! At the last moment, enlightenment has supposedly dawned on these embodiments of baseness!... Here come the shameless deceivers to inquire after one's sleep! Prestige must not be spoiled—even if the son is ruined, never mind, says the mother!... Public support must not decline—the name of son must not change—there is no concern for the son... yet these great good people have come to console their daughter-in-law! Shameless people—they danced, they sang, they enjoyed bliss, it seems—and once the emblem of that pleasure was born, like Viswamitra and Menaka they protected their empty prestige!—wretches!... The blessed souls who once threw that same veena into the ruins have now come to join its snapped string!... Leave this place; go away now! My Muthan is not here to answer all of you! If not today, tomorrow—not only to you but to worthies like you—a sword is growing in my womb to give the answer! A fire to devour false prestige!... A spear to destroy the mad elephants of religion that live by deceiving people is growing in my womb—it will answer the cheats!... answer the conspirators!... a fitting answer! a Self-Respect answer!... Wait for that answer, sinners!... Sinners who unjustly killed my Muthan! Wait for that answer—

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u007 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்:**  
Muthayi! Muthan will not die...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u008 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Is that philosophy spoken in a swami's style?... Are you saying my Muthan will live happily in heaven?... Are you saying only the flower-body perishes, while the body of fame never perishes!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u009 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாய்:**  
No! Baladevar has gone to meet the emperor—Muthan is certain to be released!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u010 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுகதேவ்:**  
There!... Father has come too. What happened, father? Is Muthan to be released?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u011 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Baladevar lowers his head.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u012 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record:ammaiyappan-s058-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தாயி:**  
Yes, freedom for my Muthan! Freedom from this world for my beloved Muthan! Freedom from this earth where traitors like you live for my dear Muthan! Freedom! Freedom!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s058-u013 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She runs off... like one seized by frenzy.]*

---

## Archival scene 59

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u001 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The door opens! Muthayi runs in.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u002 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Aththan!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u003 | source:PDF 103 / printed 101 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
My joy! Do not be shaken! Today is our last day! Our last meeting!... I can no longer see your eyes that sing of love! I can no longer taste the sweetness of your honey-pouring lips! Only another second or two; after that this poor Muthan can no longer carry within him the sweet memories of you!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u004 | source:PDF 104 / printed 102 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Aththan! No one can ever separate us—I too will come right behind you......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u005 | source:PDF 104 / printed 102 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Hmm... madness! Some wishful fool scribbled that a world has been built for lovers and that dead lovers can settle in that world! Do not believe that and follow me! I, who am about to give up my life, ask of you only one loving command...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u006 | source:PDF 104 / printed 102 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Whatever it is, ask me...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u007 | source:PDF 104 / printed 102 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
That you must live, without dying...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u008 | source:PDF 104 / printed 102 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah!... I am one who cannot live, Aththan—I must not live any longer...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u009 | source:PDF 104 / printed 102 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Hmm... You must not live? All kinds of people live in this wide world—are you alone not to live? The one made of baseness—the one of deceitful character—the one who breaks integrity—the one who breaks affection—the arrogant man lives too, my beauty! Are you alone not to live? The blood-mad man who raves ‘war, war’ lives too, my painted doll! Are you alone not to live? My treasure! In this world the treacherous man whose belly burns at seeing others live also lives—the heartless cruel man also lives—my diamond full moon! Are you alone not to live? The man who says one person must labour and another must flourish, while he himself came by without labour, lives happily too, Anjugame! Who said you must not live?... My life! Maykkainatha Swami, the silk-pitambara mendicant who deceives the town saying, ‘I am the Lord who performs the tandava—therefore fill this alms bowl,’ lives too—with drums and clamour and a thousand flag-bearers!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u010 | source:PDF 105 / printed 103 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-r001 | occurrence: | origin:source-context-attributed -->
**[முத்தன் — context-attributed; no printed speaker label]**  
Those snakes may live, tender vine! Why should you not live?... The Vengai ruler—the megalomaniac who turned beautiful Tirunagar into Pazhuthar, plundered its gold and goods and, not satisfied with that, enslaved the very soil—lives with his henchmen and immense influence! Tribute-payers—kavadi-bearers—heralds—those who make a living washing feet—all live happily, beautiful-eyed one, on this earth!... Moon-lotus! Who said that you alone must not live?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u011 | source:PDF 105 / printed 103 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Can a deer live in a tiger forest, Aththan?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u012 | source:PDF 105 / printed 103 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Honour is sharper than a tiger's claw, flower-vine! Songbirds that sing in the forest must live among eagles and vultures, my love! After I am gone, you too must live among this crowd of people without virtue. You must live not for yourself, but for the treasure of our love growing in your womb—the child, the fruit of nectar—the speaking golden picture. Even if I cannot see it, you are the one who will behold and rejoice in it; you are the one who must protect it!... My life, that child of joy is the one who will proclaim loudly into the ear of this world that Muthan's lineage has not been wiped out root and branch! Thinking of that future, even now I let out a sigh of happiness...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u013 | source:PDF 105 / printed 103 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Aththan....

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u014 | source:PDF 105 / printed 103 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
Do not cry, my dear! Do not cry! Let me see you once with a smiling face, so my heart may be cooled. Come, my Senthamizh! Show me a smile...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u015 | source:PDF 105 / printed 103 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d013 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Aththan...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u016 | source:PDF 106 / printed 104 | record:ammaiyappan-s059-d014 | occurrence:ammaiyappan-song-005 | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தன்:**  
I kissed your honey-dripping lips. The world did not like it—justice, fairness—so now I am going to kiss honour! One last thing I ask, Muthayi; one last thing I ask! That love song the two of us used to sing together—where will I ever hear it again? Come, sing it once!... Sing, my dear! If you do not sing, your lover will not die in peace... Sing, my dear, sing!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s059-u017 | source:PDF 106 / printed 104 | record: | occurrence:ammaiyappan-song-005 | origin: -->
*Song / performance reference*  
> [She sings through her tears. He keeps walking toward the gallows.]

---

## Archival scene 60

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s060-u001 | source:PDF 106 / printed 104 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The king of Vengaiyur is there, with Poongavanam nearby. Physician Boopathi speaks while looking at a lotus made of gold.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s060-u002 | source:PDF 106 / printed 104 | record:ammaiyappan-s060-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
A beautiful lotus flower—the sculptor has made it wonderfully... Its golden petals glitter as though they would seize the eye.... Yet this flower has no fragrance, Maharaja!... That is what Princess Poongavanam's life has become!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s060-u003 | source:PDF 106 / printed 104 | record:ammaiyappan-s060-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்காவனம்:**  
Maharaja!... After Physician Boopathi has told you so much, do you now understand why I refused to marry you?

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s060-u004 | source:PDF 106 / printed 104 | record:ammaiyappan-s060-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**அரச:**  
I understand, Poongavanam! If you had revealed this reason earlier, how much more peaceful my life would have been!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s060-u005 | source:PDF 107 / printed 105 | record:ammaiyappan-s060-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Maharaja! Forgive me. I have done you a great wrong. Not only to you; I have wronged womankind itself! Maharaja, somehow set my son free!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s060-u006 | source:PDF 107 / printed 105 | record:ammaiyappan-s060-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**அர:**  
Poongavanam! For the love I bore you alone, I could release Muthan. But Muthan is an enemy of Vengai Nadu!... a seeker of freedom!... There is no law by which I can release him......

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s060-u007 | source:PDF 107 / printed 105 | record:ammaiyappan-s060-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Maharaja!... Must Muthan die?... Must my son die?... Then kill me too...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s060-u008 | source:PDF 107 / printed 105 | record:ammaiyappan-s060-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**அர:**  
Do not grieve! Muthan is being released because he is the son of the woman who has lived in my heart all this time!...

---

## Archival scene 61

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s061-u001 | source:PDF 107 / printed 105 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthan is being taken to the gallows... A huge crowd. Muthan's friends are among them. Maappillaithaasu Swamigal, Baladevar and Sukhadev are also there. Muthan's friends communicate by signs.... Suddenly confusion breaks out in the crowd. Muthan's friends take Muthan away. The confusion subsides. The soldiers, mistaking a black-masked prisoner for Muthan, drag him toward the gallows.]*

---

## Archival scene 62

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s062-u001 | source:PDF 107 / printed 105 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[As the masked figure is being hanged, the rope is cut. Poongavanam runs in.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s062-u002 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record:ammaiyappan-s062-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Muthaa!... Muthaa!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s062-u003 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[She removes the cloth covering the face. Maappillaithaasar is revealed there.... She cries out.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s062-u004 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record:ammaiyappan-s062-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
What is this? The swami instead of Muthan?... How did this happen?...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s062-u005 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record:ammaiyappan-s062-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**சுக:**  
Elder!... I will tell you! I know how the swami got changed!... Muthan's friends freed Muthan, stripped off his black clothes, threw them away and ran... Seeing that, the swami immediately put on those clothes himself and stood like Muthan... These soldiers were deceived and, thinking the swami was Muthan—like this—this way—it happened.

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s062-u006 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record:ammaiyappan-s062-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**மாப்பிள்ளை:**  
No one need be surprised! This is the end I sought for myself. I have made atonement for my crimes. This is the first and last sacrifice I make for my son! On the earth where Pattinathar, the eighteen Siddhars, and virtuous ascetics lived, I came as a stain; here, I perish! Poongavanam! Poongavanam! Forgive me! Forgive me! All of you, forgive me! Forgive me!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s062-u007 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[The speech ends; the swami's life ends.]*

---

## Archival scene 63

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u001 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Muthayi lies fallen. Muthan and his comrades arrive.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u002 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d001 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Muthayi! Muthayi!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u003 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d002 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தா:**  
Aththan!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u004 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d003 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
My jewel!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u005 | source:PDF 108 / printed 106 | record: | occurrence: | origin: -->
*[Poongavanam, Baladevar, Boopathi and Sukhadev arrive.]*

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u006 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d004 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Muthaa...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u007 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d005 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Princess...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u008 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d006 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Muthaa!... The princess is your mother!... The mother who gave birth to you!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u009 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d007 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
Ah...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u010 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d008 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்தா:**  
Yes, Aththan! She is your mother!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u011 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d009 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
My mother?.... Amma!... How wrongly I thought of you in every way—forgive me, Amma! Forgive me!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u012 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d010 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூங்:**  
Muthaa!...

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u013 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d011 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**பூபதி:**  
Muthaa!... Love and motherhood have won!... Now you must win in duty!... Karmaveeran, raised together with tears! You are the commander worthy of fighting for the liberation of the motherland!... Muthayi, you and your husband must labour for the liberation of Pazhuthar... Luxury in the grove, in the breeze! Then—swing the sword to defend your own country!

<!-- unit:ammaiyappan-en-s063-u014 | source:PDF 109 / printed 107 | record:ammaiyappan-s063-d012 | occurrence: | origin:source-explicit-colon -->
**முத்:**  
I know, sir!

In love, in its ripening,
the talk is of going to battle!...
For husband and wife,
the motherland itself is breath!...

Dear comrades! Beloved younger brothers!... Everyone, set out!... Let us break Annal's shackles! Let us redeem Pazhuthar! Come! He who has a younger brother fears no army! If this army is defeated, what army can win!... Come! Come!

---
